## 39. 33-clockin-verification.sql

`sql/33-clockin-verification.sql` · 347 lines · md5 `6b2d6332be7895b5141ece68d2ad32af`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 33 · Verified clock-in: restaurant network + full-body uniform photo
--
-- Requires 29 (client_ip), 29b (add_permission, audit_row), 30
-- (site_settings).
--
-- Decided with the owner:
--  • Network. A browser cannot see which Wi-Fi router it is on, so the
--    server checks the connection's public IP against the restaurant's
--    trusted networks. "Adaptive": a manager taps "Trust this network"
--    while at the restaurant. A clock-in from anywhere else is NOT
--    refused — it is recorded as needs_approval and lands in the
--    manager's queue, so a PLDT/Globe IP change never blocks a shift; the
--    manager approves and can trust the new IP in one tap.
--    IPv4 is matched exactly; IPv6 on the /64 (phones rotate the rest).
--  • Photo. A live-camera, full-body photo at clock-in, stored in a
--    PRIVATE bucket, readable only by managers (attendance.verify),
--    deleted after 60 days (Data Privacy Act: purpose + retention are
--    shown to staff before their first photo). No photo (camera denied,
--    broken phone) → needs_approval, never a dead end.
--
-- Also closes a gap: staff could UPDATE their own open shift row, which
-- included clock_in_at — a back-dated clock-in. Now clock-in and
-- clock-out are server functions that set the time and IP themselves;
-- staff have no direct write on attendance. Manager corrections are
-- unchanged.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

select private.add_permission('attendance.verify', 'People', 'Review clock-in photos', 'Suriin ang clock-in',
  'See clock-in photos and network checks; approve or flag; trust the restaurant network.', 'manager', 215);

-- ── Trusted networks ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists public.trusted_networks (
  id           uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  branch_id    uuid not null references public.branches(id) on delete cascade,
  label        text not null default 'Restaurant Wi-Fi' check (length(label) between 1 and 60),
  network      cidr not null,
  is_active    boolean not null default true,
  added_by     uuid references public.staff(id),
  created_at   timestamptz not null default now(),
  last_seen_at timestamptz,
  unique (branch_id, network)
);
alter table public.trusted_networks enable row level security;
revoke all on public.trusted_networks from anon, authenticated;
grant select, update, delete on public.trusted_networks to authenticated;
drop policy if exists trusted_networks_manage on public.trusted_networks;
create policy trusted_networks_manage on public.trusted_networks for all to authenticated
  using (private.can('attendance.verify') and (branch_id = private.my_branch() or private.has_role('admin')))
  with check (private.can('attendance.verify') and (branch_id = private.my_branch() or private.has_role('admin')));
drop trigger if exists trg_audit_trusted_networks on public.trusted_networks;
create trigger trg_audit_trusted_networks after insert or update or delete on public.trusted_networks
  for each row execute function private.audit_row();

-- The network an address belongs to, as we store it.
create or replace function private.network_of(p_ip inet)
returns cidr language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$
  select case when p_ip is null then null
              when family(p_ip) = 4 then set_masklen(p_ip::cidr, 32)
              else network(set_masklen(p_ip, 64))::cidr end
$$;
revoke all on function private.network_of(inet) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- ── Attendance: verification columns ─────────────────────────
alter table public.attendance
  add column if not exists clock_in_ip     inet,
  add column if not exists clock_out_ip    inet,
  add column if not exists network_trusted boolean,
  add column if not exists verify_status   text
      check (verify_status is null or verify_status in ('verified', 'needs_approval', 'approved', 'flagged')),
  add column if not exists verify_reasons  text[] not null default '{}',
  add column if not exists photo_path      text,
  add column if not exists photo_purged_at timestamptz,
  add column if not exists uniform_check   jsonb,
  add column if not exists reviewed_by     uuid references public.staff(id),
  add column if not exists reviewed_at     timestamptz,
  add column if not exists review_note     text check (length(review_note) <= 500);
create index if not exists idx_attendance_review on public.attendance (verify_status) where verify_status = 'needs_approval';

-- Staff lose direct writes; managers keep the correction path.
drop policy if exists attendance_self_insert on public.attendance;
drop policy if exists attendance_self_write on public.attendance;   -- live-only duplicate of self_insert
drop policy if exists attendance_self_update on public.attendance;
drop policy if exists attendance_manager_update on public.attendance;
create policy attendance_manager_update on public.attendance for update to authenticated
  using (private.has_role('manager') and exists (select 1 from public.staff s
           where s.id = attendance.staff_id and (s.branch_id = private.my_branch() or private.has_role('admin'))))
  with check (private.has_role('manager'));
revoke insert on public.attendance from authenticated;

-- Default uniform checklist (editable in Website → settings by managers).
insert into public.site_settings (key, value) values
  ('hr.uniform_checklist', '["Uniform shirt", "Apron", "Cap or hairnet", "Closed shoes", "Name tag"]'),
  ('hr.photo_retention_days', '60')
on conflict (key) do nothing;

-- ── Clock in ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Returns the new row's id, status, reasons and — when a photo is coming —
-- the exact storage path the phone must upload to.
create or replace function public.clock_in_verified(p_expect_photo boolean default true, p_note text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  me       public.staff%rowtype;
  v_ip     inet := private.client_ip();
  v_net    cidr := private.network_of(v_ip);
  v_trust  boolean;
  v_reason text[] := '{}';
  v_id     uuid;
  v_path   text;
begin
  select * into me from public.staff where id = auth.uid();
  if not found or coalesce(me.employment_status, 'active') <> 'active' then
    raise exception 'account_not_active' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from public.attendance where staff_id = me.id and clock_out_at is null) then
    raise exception 'already_clocked_in';
  end if;

  select true into v_trust from public.trusted_networks t
   where t.branch_id = me.branch_id and t.is_active and v_net is not null and t.network >>= v_ip limit 1;
  v_trust := coalesce(v_trust, false);
  if v_trust then
    update public.trusted_networks set last_seen_at = now()
     where branch_id = me.branch_id and is_active and network >>= v_ip;
  else
    v_reason := array_append(v_reason, case when v_ip is null then 'network_unknown' else 'untrusted_network' end);
  end if;
  if not coalesce(p_expect_photo, false) then v_reason := array_append(v_reason, 'no_photo'); end if;

  insert into public.attendance (staff_id, clock_in_at, notes, clock_in_ip, network_trusted, verify_status, verify_reasons)
  values (me.id, now(), left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_note, '')), ''), 300), v_ip, v_trust,
          -- Never 'verified' at this point: that needs the photo too
          -- (attach_clockin_photo) on a trusted network.
          'needs_approval',
          case when coalesce(p_expect_photo, false) then array_append(v_reason, 'photo_pending') else v_reason end)
  returning id into v_id;

  if coalesce(p_expect_photo, false) then
    v_path := coalesce(me.branch_id::text, 'nobranch') || '/' || to_char(now() at time zone 'Asia/Manila', 'YYYY-MM-DD')
              || '/' || me.id::text || '/' || v_id::text || '.jpg';
  end if;

  insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
  values (me.id, me.role, 'attendance.clock_in', 'attendance', v_id,
          jsonb_build_object('ip', host(v_ip), 'trusted', v_trust, 'photo_expected', p_expect_photo));

  return jsonb_build_object('attendance_id', v_id, 'trusted_network', v_trust,
                            'reasons', v_reason, 'upload_path', v_path, 'clock_in_at', now());
end $$;

-- After the upload: the photo must really be in the bucket at the path we
-- issued, uploaded by this person, within 10 minutes of clocking in.
create or replace function public.attach_clockin_photo(p_attendance_id uuid)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare a public.attendance%rowtype; v_path text; v_status text; v_reasons text[];
begin
  select * into a from public.attendance where id = p_attendance_id and staff_id = auth.uid() for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'attendance_not_found'; end if;
  if a.photo_path is not null then return jsonb_build_object('status', a.verify_status); end if;
  if now() - a.clock_in_at > interval '10 minutes' then raise exception 'photo_too_late'; end if;
  select o.name into v_path from storage.objects o
   where o.bucket_id = 'attendance-photos' and o.name like '%/' || a.id::text || '.jpg'
     and split_part(o.name, '/', 3) = auth.uid()::text
   limit 1;
  if v_path is null then raise exception 'photo_not_uploaded'; end if;
  v_reasons := array_remove(a.verify_reasons, 'photo_pending');
  -- Trusted network + photo = verified. A manager still sees the photo in
  -- the review list and can flag it, but nobody has to act.
  v_status := case when a.network_trusted and cardinality(v_reasons) = 0 then 'verified' else 'needs_approval' end;
  update public.attendance set photo_path = v_path, verify_reasons = v_reasons, verify_status = v_status
   where id = a.id;
  return jsonb_build_object('status', v_status, 'reasons', v_reasons);
end $$;

-- ── Clock out ────────────────────────────────────────────────
create or replace function public.clock_out_verified()
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare a public.attendance%rowtype;
begin
  select * into a from public.attendance where staff_id = auth.uid() and clock_out_at is null
   order by clock_in_at desc limit 1 for update;
  if not found then return null; end if;
  update public.attendance set clock_out_at = now(), clock_out_ip = private.client_ip() where id = a.id;
  insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
  values (auth.uid(), private.my_role(), 'attendance.clock_out', 'attendance', a.id,
          jsonb_build_object('ip', host(private.client_ip())));
  return jsonb_build_object('attendance_id', a.id, 'clock_out_at', now());
end $$;

-- ── Manager review ───────────────────────────────────────────
create or replace function public.clockin_review_queue(p_include_done boolean default false, p_days int default 7)
returns table (id uuid, staff_id uuid, staff_name text, role public.staff_role, clock_in_at timestamptz,
               clock_out_at timestamptz, ip text, network_trusted boolean, verify_status text,
               verify_reasons text[], photo_path text, photo_purged boolean, uniform_check jsonb,
               reviewed_by_name text, reviewed_at timestamptz, review_note text)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if not private.can('attendance.verify') then raise exception 'permission_denied: attendance.verify' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query
  select a.id, a.staff_id, s.full_name, s.role, a.clock_in_at, a.clock_out_at, host(a.clock_in_ip),
         a.network_trusted, a.verify_status, a.verify_reasons, a.photo_path, a.photo_purged_at is not null,
         a.uniform_check, r.full_name, a.reviewed_at, a.review_note
    from public.attendance a
    join public.staff s on s.id = a.staff_id
    left join public.staff r on r.id = a.reviewed_by
   where (s.branch_id = private.my_branch() or private.has_role('admin'))
     and a.verify_status is not null
     and (p_include_done or a.verify_status = 'needs_approval')
     and a.clock_in_at > now() - make_interval(days => greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_days, 7), 90)))
   order by a.clock_in_at desc;
end $$;

create or replace function public.review_clockin(p_attendance_id uuid, p_approve boolean, p_uniform jsonb default null,
                                                 p_note text default null, p_trust_network boolean default false)
returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare a public.attendance%rowtype; s public.staff%rowtype; v text;
begin
  if not private.can('attendance.verify') then raise exception 'permission_denied: attendance.verify' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into a from public.attendance where id = p_attendance_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'attendance_not_found'; end if;
  select * into s from public.staff where id = a.staff_id;
  if s.branch_id is distinct from private.my_branch() and not private.has_role('admin') then
    raise exception 'wrong_branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if a.staff_id = auth.uid() and not private.has_role('director') then
    raise exception 'cannot_review_own_clockin';
  end if;
  if not p_approve and coalesce(length(btrim(p_note)), 0) < 3 then raise exception 'note_required'; end if;
  v := case when p_approve then 'approved' else 'flagged' end;
  update public.attendance
     set verify_status = v, uniform_check = p_uniform, reviewed_by = auth.uid(), reviewed_at = now(),
         review_note = left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_note, '')), ''), 500)
   where id = a.id;
  if p_trust_network and a.clock_in_ip is not null then
    insert into public.trusted_networks (branch_id, label, network, added_by)
    values (s.branch_id, 'Approved from clock-in ' || to_char(a.clock_in_at at time zone 'Asia/Manila', 'Mon DD'),
            private.network_of(a.clock_in_ip), auth.uid())
    on conflict (branch_id, network) do update set is_active = true;
  end if;
  if not p_approve then
    insert into public.notifications (user_id, branch_id, kind, title, body, priority, entity_type, entity_id)
    values (a.staff_id, s.branch_id, 'attendance', 'Your clock-in was flagged',
            left(btrim(p_note), 300), 'high', 'attendance', a.id);
  end if;
  return v;
end $$;

-- Trust the network the manager is on right now (at the restaurant).
create or replace function public.trust_current_network(p_label text default 'Restaurant Wi-Fi')
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare v_ip inet := private.client_ip(); v_branch uuid := private.my_branch(); v_id uuid;
begin
  if not private.can('attendance.verify') then raise exception 'permission_denied: attendance.verify' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if v_ip is null then raise exception 'network_unknown'; end if;
  insert into public.trusted_networks (branch_id, label, network, added_by)
  values (v_branch, left(coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_label), ''), 'Restaurant Wi-Fi'), 60), private.network_of(v_ip), auth.uid())
  on conflict (branch_id, network) do update set is_active = true, label = excluded.label
  returning id into v_id;
  return jsonb_build_object('id', v_id, 'network', host(private.network_of(v_ip)), 'family', family(v_ip));
end $$;

-- What network am I on, and is it trusted? (Shown on the clock-in screen.)
create or replace function public.my_network_status()
returns jsonb
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'trusted', exists (select 1 from public.trusted_networks t
                        where t.branch_id = private.my_branch() and t.is_active and t.network >>= private.client_ip()),
    'any_trusted', exists (select 1 from public.trusted_networks t where t.branch_id = private.my_branch() and t.is_active))
$$;

-- Photos older than the retention window: list them for deletion (the
-- portal deletes the files through the Storage API, which is the only
-- supported way), then mark them purged.
create or replace function public.clockin_photos_expired(p_limit int default 200)
returns table (attendance_id uuid, photo_path text)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare v_days int := coalesce((select (value #>> '{}')::int from public.site_settings where key = 'hr.photo_retention_days'), 60);
begin
  if not private.can('attendance.verify') then raise exception 'permission_denied' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query select a.id, a.photo_path from public.attendance a
    where a.photo_path is not null and a.photo_purged_at is null
      and a.clock_in_at < now() - make_interval(days => greatest(v_days, 7))
    order by a.clock_in_at limit greatest(1, least(p_limit, 1000));
end $$;
create or replace function public.mark_clockin_photos_purged(p_ids uuid[])
returns int
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare n int;
begin
  if not private.can('attendance.verify') then raise exception 'permission_denied' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  update public.attendance set photo_purged_at = now() where id = any(p_ids) and photo_path is not null and photo_purged_at is null;
  get diagnostics n = row_count;
  return n;
end $$;

do $$
declare f text;
begin
  foreach f in array array[
    'public.clock_in_verified(boolean, text)', 'public.attach_clockin_photo(uuid)', 'public.clock_out_verified()',
    'public.clockin_review_queue(boolean, int)', 'public.review_clockin(uuid, boolean, jsonb, text, boolean)',
    'public.trust_current_network(text)', 'public.my_network_status()',
    'public.clockin_photos_expired(int)', 'public.mark_clockin_photos_purged(uuid[])'] loop
    execute 'revoke all on function ' || f || ' from public, anon';
    execute 'grant execute on function ' || f || ' to authenticated';
  end loop;
end $$;

-- ── Storage: private bucket + policies ───────────────────────
insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
values ('attendance-photos', 'attendance-photos', false, 1048576, array['image/jpeg'])
on conflict (id) do update set public = false, file_size_limit = 1048576, allowed_mime_types = array['image/jpeg'];

-- Staff may upload ONE photo, only to the path issued for their own open
-- clock-in (…/<their id>/<attendance id>.jpg), within 10 minutes.
drop policy if exists clockin_photo_upload on storage.objects;
create policy clockin_photo_upload on storage.objects for insert to authenticated
  with check (
    bucket_id = 'attendance-photos'
    and split_part(name, '/', 3) = auth.uid()::text
    and exists (select 1 from public.attendance a
                 where a.staff_id = auth.uid()
                   and name like '%/' || a.id::text || '.jpg'
                   and a.photo_path is null
                   and a.clock_in_at > now() - interval '10 minutes')
  );
drop policy if exists clockin_photo_read on storage.objects;
create policy clockin_photo_read on storage.objects for select to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'attendance-photos' and private.can('attendance.verify'));
drop policy if exists clockin_photo_delete on storage.objects;
create policy clockin_photo_delete on storage.objects for delete to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'attendance-photos' and private.can('attendance.verify'));
````

## 40. 34-stamp-cards.sql

`sql/34-stamp-cards.sql` · 331 lines · md5 `e229c50bdefee28b13e6fa2697b6d341`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 34 · Digital stamp cards (hard to fake)
--
-- Requires 29 (track_token, rate_limit), 29b, 30 (site_settings), 31
-- (payment_status is derived from real payments).
--
-- How it resists cheating:
--  • Stamps are written ONLY by a trigger when an order's payment_status
--    becomes 'paid' (which sql/31 only does when confirmed money covers
--    the bill) and the total is at least the minimum. One per order.
--    A refund or cancellation writes a reversal. No client can insert.
--  • A card is the customer's phone number, stored only as a salted
--    hash plus the last 4 digits. Nobody can look a card up by phone from
--    the outside: claiming a card on a device needs proof of purchase —
--    the private tracking token of a paid order on that phone (sql/29).
--  • The device gets a random secret (stored hashed). Viewing the card
--    needs it.
--  • Redeeming is staff-only (rewards.redeem). The customer shows a QR /
--    6-digit code that changes every 30 seconds, derived from a server
--    secret; each code works once. A screenshot is useless half a minute
--    later.
--
-- Settings (site_settings, editable in the Rewards tab):
--   rewards.enabled, rewards.min_order, rewards.stamps_needed,
--   rewards.reward_text, rewards.card_days (stamps expire after N days)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

select private.add_permission('rewards.redeem', 'Money', 'Redeem stamp-card rewards', 'Mag-redeem ng reward',
  'Scan a customer''s rotating rewards code and give the reward.', 'supervisor', 350);
select private.add_permission('rewards.manage', 'Money', 'Manage the stamp card', 'Pamahalaan ang stamp card',
  'Change the minimum order, stamps needed and the reward.', 'manager', 351);

insert into public.site_settings (key, value) values
  ('rewards.enabled',       'false'),
  ('rewards.min_order',     '300'),
  ('rewards.stamps_needed', '10'),
  ('rewards.reward_text',   '"Free dish of your choice (up to ₱300)"'),
  ('rewards.card_days',     '365')
on conflict (key) do nothing;

-- Server secret for codes and phone hashing. Lives in private, which the
-- API never exposes. Generated once.
create table if not exists private.loyalty_secret (
  id     int primary key default 1 check (id = 1),
  secret text not null
);
insert into private.loyalty_secret (secret)
select encode(sha256((gen_random_uuid()::text || clock_timestamp()::text || random()::text)::bytea), 'hex')
where not exists (select 1 from private.loyalty_secret);

create or replace function private.loyalty_hash(p text)
returns text language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select encode(sha256(((select secret from private.loyalty_secret) || ':' || p)::bytea), 'hex')
$$;
create or replace function private.norm_phone(p text)
returns text language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$
  -- 09171234567 / +639171234567 / 639171234567 / 9171234567 → +639171234567
  select case
    when d ~ '^09\d{9}$'  then '+63' || substr(d, 2)
    when d ~ '^639\d{9}$' then '+' || d
    when d ~ '^9\d{9}$'   then '+63' || d
    else null end
  from (select regexp_replace(coalesce(p, ''), '\D', '', 'g') as d) x
$$;
create or replace function private.setting_num(k text, dflt numeric)
returns numeric language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select coalesce((select (value #>> '{}')::numeric from public.site_settings where key = k), dflt)
$$;
create or replace function private.setting_bool(k text)
returns boolean language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select coalesce((select (value #>> '{}') in ('true', 'yes', '1') from public.site_settings where key = k), false)
$$;
revoke all on function private.loyalty_hash(text) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function private.norm_phone(text) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function private.setting_num(text, numeric) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function private.setting_bool(text) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- ── Tables ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists public.loyalty_cards (
  id          uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  phone_hash  text not null unique,
  phone_last4 text not null check (phone_last4 ~ '^\d{4}$'),
  created_at  timestamptz not null default now()
);
create table if not exists public.loyalty_devices (
  id           uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  card_id      uuid not null references public.loyalty_cards(id) on delete cascade,
  secret_hash  text not null unique,
  created_at   timestamptz not null default now(),
  last_seen_at timestamptz,
  revoked_at   timestamptz
);
create table if not exists public.loyalty_ledger (
  id          uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  card_id     uuid not null references public.loyalty_cards(id) on delete cascade,
  order_id    uuid references public.orders(id) on delete set null,
  kind        text not null check (kind in ('stamp', 'reversal', 'redeem', 'adjust')),
  delta       int not null,
  note        text check (length(note) <= 200),
  created_by  uuid references public.staff(id),
  created_at  timestamptz not null default now(),
  expires_at  timestamptz
);
create unique index if not exists uq_ledger_order_kind on public.loyalty_ledger (order_id, kind) where order_id is not null;
create index if not exists idx_ledger_card on public.loyalty_ledger (card_id, created_at desc);
create table if not exists private.loyalty_used_codes (
  card_id uuid not null,
  win     bigint not null,
  used_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (card_id, win)
);

do $$
declare t text;
begin
  foreach t in array array['loyalty_cards', 'loyalty_devices', 'loyalty_ledger'] loop
    execute format('alter table public.%I enable row level security', t);
    execute format('revoke all on public.%I from anon, authenticated', t);
    execute format('grant select on public.%I to authenticated', t);
    execute format('drop policy if exists %1$s_staff_read on public.%1$I', t);
    execute format('create policy %1$s_staff_read on public.%1$I for select to authenticated using (private.can(''rewards.redeem'') or private.can(''rewards.manage''))', t);
  end loop;
end $$;
drop trigger if exists trg_audit_loyalty_ledger on public.loyalty_ledger;
create trigger trg_audit_loyalty_ledger after insert on public.loyalty_ledger
  for each row execute function private.audit_row();

-- Balance counts only unexpired stamps; redemptions spend the oldest first
-- simply by being negative.
create or replace function private.card_balance(p_card uuid)
returns int language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select coalesce(sum(delta) filter (where expires_at is null or expires_at > now()), 0)::int
    from public.loyalty_ledger where card_id = p_card
$$;
revoke all on function private.card_balance(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.card_for_phone(p_phone text, p_create boolean)
returns uuid language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_norm text := private.norm_phone(p_phone); v_id uuid;
begin
  if v_norm is null then return null; end if;
  select id into v_id from public.loyalty_cards where phone_hash = private.loyalty_hash(v_norm);
  if v_id is null and p_create then
    insert into public.loyalty_cards (phone_hash, phone_last4) values (private.loyalty_hash(v_norm), right(v_norm, 4))
    on conflict (phone_hash) do update set phone_hash = excluded.phone_hash
    returning id into v_id;
  end if;
  return v_id;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.card_for_phone(text, boolean) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- ── The only way a stamp appears ─────────────────────────────
create or replace function private.trg_order_stamp()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_card uuid; v_days int;
begin
  -- Earned: became paid.
  if new.payment_status = 'paid' and old.payment_status is distinct from 'paid'
     and private.setting_bool('rewards.enabled')
     and new.total >= private.setting_num('rewards.min_order', 300)
     and new.customer_phone is not null then
    v_card := private.card_for_phone(new.customer_phone, true);
    if v_card is not null then
      v_days := private.setting_num('rewards.card_days', 365)::int;
      insert into public.loyalty_ledger (card_id, order_id, kind, delta, expires_at)
      values (v_card, new.id, 'stamp', 1, case when v_days > 0 then now() + make_interval(days => v_days) end)
      on conflict do nothing;
    end if;
  end if;
  -- Taken back: refunded or cancelled after a stamp was given.
  if (new.payment_status = 'refunded' and old.payment_status is distinct from 'refunded')
     or (new.status = 'cancelled' and old.status is distinct from 'cancelled') then
    insert into public.loyalty_ledger (card_id, order_id, kind, delta, note)
    select l.card_id, new.id, 'reversal', -1, 'Order ' || coalesce(new.order_number, '') || ' refunded/cancelled'
      from public.loyalty_ledger l where l.order_id = new.id and l.kind = 'stamp'
    on conflict do nothing;
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_order_stamp() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_order_stamp on public.orders;
create trigger trg_order_stamp after update of payment_status, status on public.orders
  for each row execute function private.trg_order_stamp();

-- ── Customer side (anonymous, rate limited) ──────────────────
-- Claim this phone's card on this device with proof of purchase.
create or replace function public.claim_stamp_card(p_phone text, p_order_number text, p_track_token uuid)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare o public.orders%rowtype; v_card uuid; v_secret text;
begin
  perform private.rate_limit('loyalty_claim', 10, interval '30 minutes');
  if private.norm_phone(p_phone) is null then raise exception 'bad_phone'; end if;
  select * into o from public.orders
   where upper(order_number) = upper(btrim(p_order_number)) and track_token = p_track_token;
  if not found then raise exception 'proof_not_found'; end if;
  if private.norm_phone(o.customer_phone) is distinct from private.norm_phone(p_phone) then
    raise exception 'phone_does_not_match_order';
  end if;
  v_card := private.card_for_phone(p_phone, true);
  v_secret := replace(gen_random_uuid()::text || gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', '');
  insert into public.loyalty_devices (card_id, secret_hash) values (v_card, private.loyalty_hash('dev:' || v_secret));
  return jsonb_build_object('card_id', v_card, 'device_secret', v_secret);
end $$;

create or replace function private.card_for_device(p_card uuid, p_secret text)
returns uuid language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v uuid;
begin
  update public.loyalty_devices set last_seen_at = now()
   where card_id = p_card and secret_hash = private.loyalty_hash('dev:' || coalesce(p_secret, '')) and revoked_at is null
  returning card_id into v;
  if v is null then raise exception 'card_not_on_this_device'; end if;
  return v;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.card_for_device(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function public.my_stamp_card(p_card uuid, p_secret text)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v uuid;
begin
  perform private.rate_limit('loyalty_view', 120, interval '10 minutes');
  v := private.card_for_device(p_card, p_secret);
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'card_id', v, 'last4', (select phone_last4 from public.loyalty_cards where id = v),
    'stamps', private.card_balance(v),
    'needed', private.setting_num('rewards.stamps_needed', 10)::int,
    'reward', (select value #>> '{}' from public.site_settings where key = 'rewards.reward_text'),
    'min_order', private.setting_num('rewards.min_order', 300),
    'enabled', private.setting_bool('rewards.enabled'),
    'history', coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('kind', kind, 'delta', delta, 'at', created_at,
                                 'expires_at', expires_at) order by created_at desc)
                           from (select * from public.loyalty_ledger where card_id = v order by created_at desc limit 20) h), '[]'::jsonb));
end $$;

-- Rotating code: 6 digits, new every 30 seconds.
create or replace function private.card_code(p_card uuid, p_win bigint)
returns text language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select lpad((abs(('x' || substr(encode(sha256(((select secret from private.loyalty_secret) || ':code:' || p_card::text || ':' || p_win::text)::bytea), 'hex'), 1, 8))::bit(32)::bigint) % 1000000)::text, 6, '0')
$$;
revoke all on function private.card_code(uuid, bigint) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function public.stamp_card_code(p_card uuid, p_secret text)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v uuid; w bigint := floor(extract(epoch from now()) / 30);
begin
  perform private.rate_limit('loyalty_code', 240, interval '10 minutes');
  v := private.card_for_device(p_card, p_secret);
  return jsonb_build_object('card_id', v, 'code', private.card_code(v, w),
                            'valid_for', 30 - (floor(extract(epoch from now()))::bigint % 30));
end $$;

-- ── Staff side ───────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Accepts the current or previous 30-second code (a slow scan), once.
create or replace function public.redeem_stamp_reward(p_card uuid, p_code text, p_note text default null)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare w bigint := floor(extract(epoch from now()) / 30); v_win bigint; v_need int; v_bal int;
begin
  if not private.can('rewards.redeem') then raise exception 'permission_denied: rewards.redeem' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.loyalty_cards where id = p_card) then raise exception 'card_not_found'; end if;
  select x into v_win from unnest(array[w, w - 1]) x where private.card_code(p_card, x) = btrim(coalesce(p_code, '')) limit 1;
  if v_win is null then raise exception 'code_expired_or_wrong'; end if;
  begin
    insert into private.loyalty_used_codes (card_id, win) values (p_card, v_win);
  exception when unique_violation then
    raise exception 'code_already_used';
  end;
  v_need := private.setting_num('rewards.stamps_needed', 10)::int;
  v_bal := private.card_balance(p_card);
  if v_bal < v_need then raise exception 'not_enough_stamps: % of %', v_bal, v_need; end if;
  insert into public.loyalty_ledger (card_id, kind, delta, note, created_by)
  values (p_card, 'redeem', -v_need, left(coalesce(p_note, 'Reward given'), 200), auth.uid());
  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'reward', (select value #>> '{}' from public.site_settings where key = 'rewards.reward_text'),
                            'stamps_left', private.card_balance(p_card));
end $$;

-- Staff lookup by phone (for "how many stamps do I have?" at the counter).
create or replace function public.stamp_card_lookup(p_phone text)
returns jsonb language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v uuid;
begin
  if not (private.can('rewards.redeem') or private.can('rewards.manage')) then raise exception 'permission_denied' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  v := private.card_for_phone(p_phone, false);
  if v is null then return null; end if;
  return jsonb_build_object('card_id', v, 'stamps', private.card_balance(v),
    'needed', private.setting_num('rewards.stamps_needed', 10)::int,
    'devices', (select count(*) from public.loyalty_devices where card_id = v and revoked_at is null),
    'history', coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('kind', l.kind, 'delta', l.delta, 'at', l.created_at,
                 'order_number', o.order_number, 'note', l.note) order by l.created_at desc)
               from public.loyalty_ledger l left join public.orders o on o.id = l.order_id where l.card_id = v), '[]'::jsonb));
end $$;

create or replace function public.revoke_stamp_devices(p_card uuid)
returns int language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare n int;
begin
  if not private.can('rewards.manage') then raise exception 'permission_denied' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  update public.loyalty_devices set revoked_at = now() where card_id = p_card and revoked_at is null;
  get diagnostics n = row_count; return n;
end $$;

do $$
declare f text;
begin
  foreach f in array array['public.claim_stamp_card(text, text, uuid)', 'public.my_stamp_card(uuid, text)', 'public.stamp_card_code(uuid, text)'] loop
    execute 'revoke all on function ' || f || ' from public';
    execute 'grant execute on function ' || f || ' to anon, authenticated';
  end loop;
  foreach f in array array['public.redeem_stamp_reward(uuid, text, text)', 'public.stamp_card_lookup(text)', 'public.revoke_stamp_devices(uuid)'] loop
    execute 'revoke all on function ' || f || ' from public, anon';
    execute 'grant execute on function ' || f || ' to authenticated';
  end loop;
end $$;

-- Reward settings are public (the card page shows them) but only
-- rewards.manage may change them, on top of site.edit.
create or replace function private.trg_rewards_settings_guard()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if coalesce(new.key, old.key) like 'rewards.%' and auth.uid() is not null and not private.can('rewards.manage') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: rewards.manage' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return coalesce(new, old);
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_rewards_settings_guard() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_rewards_settings_guard on public.site_settings;
create trigger trg_rewards_settings_guard before insert or update or delete on public.site_settings
  for each row execute function private.trg_rewards_settings_guard();
drop policy if exists site_settings_rewards_write on public.site_settings;
create policy site_settings_rewards_write on public.site_settings for all to authenticated
  using (key like 'rewards.%' and private.can('rewards.manage'))
  with check (key like 'rewards.%' and private.can('rewards.manage'));
````

<a id="41"></a>

## 41. 35-table-settle.sql

`sql/35-table-settle.sql` · 102 lines · md5 `aca3905bef313d09cc3c38e23d876e0c`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 35 · Settle a whole table in one go (floor screen "Close table")
--
-- A dine-in table usually has several orders (each QR round is its own
-- order). Closing the table on the floor screen used to mark them
-- completed without recording any money, so they showed as unpaid in the
-- daily close and the cash never reached the drawer report.
--
--   orders_amount_due(ids)      what each order still owes (staff only)
--   settle_table_cash(ids, …)   one cash tender covers all of them, in one
--                               transaction: every order gets its own
--                               payments row for exactly what it owed, so
--                               refunds, receipts and stamps keep working
--                               per order. All or nothing.
--
-- Depends on sql/31 (payments, settle_order, amount_due) and sql/32
-- (cash sessions; the insert trigger attaches the open drawer).
-- Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

create or replace function public.orders_amount_due(p_order_ids uuid[])
returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  o     public.orders%rowtype;
  v_out jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
begin
  if not (private.can('payments.take') or private.can('orders.view')) then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_order_ids is null or cardinality(p_order_ids) = 0 then return v_out; end if;
  if cardinality(p_order_ids) > 50 then raise exception 'too_many_orders'; end if;

  for o in select * from public.orders where id = any(p_order_ids) order by created_at loop
    perform private.assert_order_access(o);
    v_out := v_out || jsonb_build_object(
      'order_id', o.id, 'order_number', o.order_number, 'status', o.status,
      'total', o.total, 'payment_status', o.payment_status,
      'amount_due', case when o.status = 'cancelled' then 0 else private.amount_due(o.id) end);
  end loop;
  return v_out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.settle_table_cash(
  p_order_ids uuid[], p_tendered numeric, p_tip numeric default 0,
  p_cash_session uuid default null, p_note text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  o        public.orders%rowtype;
  v_tip    numeric(10,2) := round(coalesce(p_tip, 0), 2);
  v_total  numeric(10,2) := 0;
  v_due    numeric(10,2);
  v_first  boolean := true;
  v_paid   int := 0;
  v_note   text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_note, '')), ''), 500);
begin
  if not private.can('payments.take') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_order_ids is null or cardinality(p_order_ids) = 0 then raise exception 'nothing_due'; end if;
  if cardinality(p_order_ids) > 50 then raise exception 'too_many_orders'; end if;
  if v_tip < 0 or v_tip > 100000 then raise exception 'bad_tip'; end if;

  -- Lock every order first (fixed order, so two screens cannot deadlock),
  -- then add up what is owed.
  for o in select * from public.orders where id = any(p_order_ids) order by id for update loop
    perform private.assert_order_access(o);
    if o.status <> 'cancelled' then v_total := v_total + private.amount_due(o.id); end if;
  end loop;
  if v_total <= 0 then raise exception 'nothing_due'; end if;
  if p_tendered is null or round(p_tendered, 2) < v_total + v_tip then
    raise exception 'not_enough_cash: need %', v_total + v_tip;
  end if;

  for o in select * from public.orders where id = any(p_order_ids) and status <> 'cancelled' order by created_at loop
    v_due := private.amount_due(o.id);
    continue when v_due <= 0;
    -- The tip and the change ride on the first row; every row's amount is
    -- exactly what that order owed.
    insert into public.payments (order_id, branch_id, method, status, amount, tip, tendered, change_due,
                                 cash_session_id, note, created_by, confirmed_by, confirmed_at)
    values (o.id, o.branch_id, 'cash', 'completed', v_due,
            case when v_first then v_tip else 0 end,
            case when v_first then round(p_tendered, 2) - (v_total - v_due) else v_due end,
            case when v_first then round(p_tendered, 2) - v_total - v_tip else 0 end,
            p_cash_session, coalesce(v_note, 'Table settled together'), auth.uid(), auth.uid(), now());
    perform private.settle_order(o.id);
    v_first := false;
    v_paid := v_paid + 1;
  end loop;

  return jsonb_build_object('orders_paid', v_paid, 'amount', v_total, 'tip', v_tip,
                            'change_due', round(p_tendered, 2) - v_total - v_tip);
end $$;

revoke all on function public.orders_amount_due(uuid[]) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.settle_table_cash(uuid[], numeric, numeric, uuid, text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.orders_amount_due(uuid[]) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.settle_table_cash(uuid[], numeric, numeric, uuid, text) to authenticated;
````

<a id="42"></a>

## 42. 36-read-receipts.sql

`sql/36-read-receipts.sql` · 143 lines · md5 `ee8ba41525c7df2d00b063592a286aca`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 36 · Read receipts per person (notifications + broadcast messages)
--
-- Two bugs shared one cause: a branch-wide notification or message is a
-- single row with a single read_at.
--   • Crew could see branch notifications but not mark them read (only a
--     manager may update them), so "read" ones came back on every load.
--   • When a manager did mark one read, it vanished for everybody; and the
--     first person to open a branch message marked it read for the branch.
-- Worse, any branch member could UPDATE a broadcast message — subject and
-- body included — because the grant and the policy were not column-bound.
--
-- Now: personal rows keep read_at; branch-wide rows get a receipt per
-- person (read, or dismissed = hidden for me). Staff may only touch
-- read_at, and only on rows addressed to them.
-- Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

create table if not exists public.read_receipts (
  kind          text not null check (kind in ('notification', 'message')),
  item_id       uuid not null,
  user_id       uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  read_at       timestamptz not null default now(),
  dismissed_at  timestamptz,
  primary key (kind, item_id, user_id)
);
create index if not exists read_receipts_user_idx on public.read_receipts (user_id, kind);
alter table public.read_receipts enable row level security;

drop policy if exists read_receipts_own on public.read_receipts;
create policy read_receipts_own on public.read_receipts for all to authenticated
  using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());
revoke all on public.read_receipts from anon;
grant select, insert, update on public.read_receipts to authenticated;

-- ── Writes on the shared rows: read_at only, own rows only ─────────
revoke update on public.messages from authenticated;
grant update (read_at) on public.messages to authenticated;
drop policy if exists messages_update_read on public.messages;      -- let any branch member edit a broadcast
-- messages_mark_read (to_staff_id = auth.uid()) stays.

revoke update on public.notifications from authenticated;
grant update (read_at) on public.notifications to authenticated;
drop policy if exists notif_update on public.notifications;
create policy notif_update on public.notifications for update to authenticated
  using (user_id = auth.uid() or private.has_role('admin'))
  with check (user_id = auth.uid() or private.has_role('admin'));

-- ── What I see ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- SECURITY INVOKER: the table policies still decide what is visible.
-- A branch row read before this migration (shared read_at) stays read.
create or replace function public.my_notifications(p_limit int default 100, p_unread_only boolean default false)
returns setof jsonb
language sql stable security invoker set search_path = ''
as $$
  select to_jsonb(n) || jsonb_build_object('read_at',
           coalesce(case when n.user_id is not null then n.read_at end, r.read_at,
                    case when n.user_id is null then n.read_at end),
           'shared', n.user_id is null)
    from public.notifications n
    left join public.read_receipts r on r.kind = 'notification' and r.item_id = n.id and r.user_id = auth.uid()
   where r.dismissed_at is null
     and (not p_unread_only or coalesce(case when n.user_id is not null then n.read_at end, r.read_at,
                                        case when n.user_id is null then n.read_at end) is null)
   order by n.created_at desc
   limit least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 100), 1), 500)
$$;

create or replace function public.my_unread_counts()
returns jsonb
language sql stable security invoker set search_path = ''
as $$
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'notifications', (select count(*) from public.notifications n
                        left join public.read_receipts r on r.kind = 'notification' and r.item_id = n.id and r.user_id = auth.uid()
                       where r.dismissed_at is null
                         and coalesce(case when n.user_id is not null then n.read_at end, r.read_at,
                                      case when n.user_id is null then n.read_at end) is null),
    'messages', (select count(*) from public.messages m
                   left join public.read_receipts r on r.kind = 'message' and r.item_id = m.id and r.user_id = auth.uid()
                  where (m.to_staff_id = auth.uid() or m.to_staff_id is null)
                    and m.from_staff_id is distinct from auth.uid()
                    and r.dismissed_at is null
                    and coalesce(case when m.to_staff_id is not null then m.read_at end, r.read_at,
                                 case when m.to_staff_id is null then m.read_at end) is null))
$$;

-- ── Mark read / dismiss ────────────────────────────────────────────
-- p_ids null = everything visible to me of that kind.
create or replace function public.mark_read(p_kind text, p_ids uuid[] default null)
returns int
language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = ''
as $$
declare v int := 0; n int;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null then raise exception 'not_signed_in' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_kind = 'notification' then
    update public.notifications set read_at = now()
     where user_id = auth.uid() and read_at is null and (p_ids is null or id = any(p_ids));
    get diagnostics n = row_count; v := v + n;
    insert into public.read_receipts (kind, item_id, user_id)
    select 'notification', id, auth.uid() from public.notifications
     where user_id is null and (p_ids is null or id = any(p_ids))
    on conflict (kind, item_id, user_id) do nothing;
    get diagnostics n = row_count; v := v + n;
  elsif p_kind = 'message' then
    update public.messages set read_at = now()
     where to_staff_id = auth.uid() and read_at is null and (p_ids is null or id = any(p_ids));
    get diagnostics n = row_count; v := v + n;
    insert into public.read_receipts (kind, item_id, user_id)
    select 'message', id, auth.uid() from public.messages
     where to_staff_id is null and (p_ids is null or id = any(p_ids))
    on conflict (kind, item_id, user_id) do nothing;
    get diagnostics n = row_count; v := v + n;
  else
    raise exception 'bad_kind';
  end if;
  return v;
end $$;

-- Mine: deleted. Branch-wide: hidden for me only.
create or replace function public.dismiss_notification(p_id uuid)
returns void
language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if auth.uid() is null then raise exception 'not_signed_in' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  delete from public.notifications where id = p_id and user_id = auth.uid();
  if not found then
    insert into public.read_receipts (kind, item_id, user_id, dismissed_at)
    select 'notification', id, auth.uid(), now() from public.notifications where id = p_id
    on conflict (kind, item_id, user_id) do update set dismissed_at = now();
  end if;
end $$;

revoke all on function public.my_notifications(int, boolean) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.my_unread_counts() from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.mark_read(text, uuid[]) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.dismiss_notification(uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.my_notifications(int, boolean) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.my_unread_counts() to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.mark_read(text, uuid[]) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.dismiss_notification(uuid) to authenticated;
````
