<a id="44"></a>

## 44. 37b-manual-payments.sql

`sql/37b-manual-payments.sql` · 163 lines · md5 `23033c8ef292e1d6b0a9f4fcc22a8f66`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 37b · Manual payments: GCash, Maya, QR Ph, card terminal, bank
--
-- The restaurant takes every non-cash payment by hand: staff show the
-- shop's QR (or run the card terminal), check it went through, and type
-- the reference / approval code. No payment app talks to the system.
--
--   record_manual_payment(order, method, reference, amount?, tip?, note?)
--   settle_table_manual(order_ids, method, reference, tip?, note?)
--
-- Both count as paid at once (the staff member is vouching for it) and
-- keep who, when and the reference, so a disputed payment can be traced.
-- Cash keeps record_cash_payment / settle_table_cash.
-- Needs 37a (enum value 'qrph'). Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- Allowed methods on the ledger.
alter table public.payments drop constraint if exists payments_method_check;
alter table public.payments add constraint payments_method_check
  check (method in ('cash', 'gcash', 'maya', 'qrph', 'card', 'bank', 'hitpay', 'card_terminal', 'other'));

-- The method that brought in the most money names the order.
create or replace function private.settle_order(p_order_id uuid)
returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  o        public.orders%rowtype;
  v_in     numeric(10,2);
  v_out    numeric(10,2);
  v_method text;
  v_status text;
begin
  select * into o from public.orders where id = p_order_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'order_not_found'; end if;

  select coalesce(sum(amount) filter (where kind = 'payment'), 0),
         coalesce(sum(amount) filter (where kind = 'refund'), 0)
    into v_in, v_out
    from public.payments where order_id = p_order_id and status = 'completed';

  v_status := case
    when v_in > 0 and v_out >= v_in               then 'refunded'
    when o.total > 0 and v_in - v_out >= o.total  then 'paid'
    when o.total = 0 and v_in > 0 and v_out = 0   then 'paid'
    else 'pending' end;

  select method into v_method from public.payments
   where order_id = p_order_id and status = 'completed' and kind = 'payment'
   group by method order by sum(amount) desc limit 1;

  perform set_config('tpn.settle', 'on', true);
  update public.orders
     set payment_status = v_status::public.payment_status,
         payment_method = coalesce(case v_method
                                     when 'cash' then 'cash' when 'hitpay' then 'hitpay' when 'bank' then 'bank'
                                     when 'card' then 'card' when 'card_terminal' then 'card'
                                     when 'gcash' then 'gcash' when 'maya' then 'paymaya' when 'qrph' then 'qrph' end,
                                   payment_method::text)::public.payment_method
   where id = p_order_id;
  perform set_config('tpn.settle', 'off', true);
  return v_status;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.settle_order(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.manual_method(p_method text)
returns text language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$
  select case lower(btrim(coalesce(p_method, '')))
    when 'gcash' then 'gcash' when 'maya' then 'maya' when 'paymaya' then 'maya'
    when 'qrph' then 'qrph' when 'qr ph' then 'qrph' when 'card' then 'card' when 'bank' then 'bank' end
$$;

create or replace function public.record_manual_payment(
  p_order_id uuid, p_method text, p_reference text,
  p_amount numeric default null, p_tip numeric default 0, p_note text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  o       public.orders%rowtype;
  v_m     text := private.manual_method(p_method);
  v_ref   text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_reference, '')), ''), 120);
  v_due   numeric(10,2);
  v_amt   numeric(10,2);
  v_tip   numeric(10,2) := round(coalesce(p_tip, 0), 2);
  v_id    uuid;
  v_state text;
begin
  if not private.can('payments.take') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_m is null then raise exception 'bad_method'; end if;
  if v_ref is null or length(v_ref) < 3 then raise exception 'reference_required'; end if;
  select * into o from public.orders where id = p_order_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'order_not_found'; end if;
  perform private.assert_order_access(o);
  if o.status = 'cancelled' then raise exception 'order_cancelled'; end if;
  v_due := private.amount_due(o.id);
  if v_due <= 0 then raise exception 'nothing_due'; end if;
  v_amt := round(coalesce(p_amount, v_due), 2);
  if v_amt <= 0 or v_amt > v_due then raise exception 'bad_amount: due is %', v_due; end if;
  if v_tip < 0 or v_tip > 100000 then raise exception 'bad_tip'; end if;

  insert into public.payments (order_id, branch_id, method, status, amount, tip, reference, note,
                               created_by, confirmed_by, confirmed_at)
  values (o.id, o.branch_id, v_m, 'completed', v_amt, v_tip, v_ref,
          left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_note, '')), ''), 500), auth.uid(), auth.uid(), now())
  returning id into v_id;

  v_state := private.settle_order(o.id);
  return jsonb_build_object('payment_id', v_id, 'payment_status', v_state, 'amount_due', private.amount_due(o.id));
end $$;

create or replace function public.settle_table_manual(
  p_order_ids uuid[], p_method text, p_reference text, p_tip numeric default 0, p_note text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  o       public.orders%rowtype;
  v_m     text := private.manual_method(p_method);
  v_ref   text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_reference, '')), ''), 120);
  v_tip   numeric(10,2) := round(coalesce(p_tip, 0), 2);
  v_total numeric(10,2) := 0;
  v_due   numeric(10,2);
  v_first boolean := true;
  v_paid  int := 0;
begin
  if not private.can('payments.take') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_m is null then raise exception 'bad_method'; end if;
  if v_ref is null or length(v_ref) < 3 then raise exception 'reference_required'; end if;
  if p_order_ids is null or cardinality(p_order_ids) = 0 then raise exception 'nothing_due'; end if;
  if cardinality(p_order_ids) > 50 then raise exception 'too_many_orders'; end if;
  if v_tip < 0 or v_tip > 100000 then raise exception 'bad_tip'; end if;

  for o in select * from public.orders where id = any(p_order_ids) order by id for update loop
    perform private.assert_order_access(o);
    if o.status <> 'cancelled' then v_total := v_total + private.amount_due(o.id); end if;
  end loop;
  if v_total <= 0 then raise exception 'nothing_due'; end if;

  for o in select * from public.orders where id = any(p_order_ids) and status <> 'cancelled' order by created_at loop
    v_due := private.amount_due(o.id);
    continue when v_due <= 0;
    insert into public.payments (order_id, branch_id, method, status, amount, tip, reference, note,
                                 created_by, confirmed_by, confirmed_at)
    values (o.id, o.branch_id, v_m, 'completed', v_due, case when v_first then v_tip else 0 end, v_ref,
            coalesce(left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_note, '')), ''), 500), 'Table settled together'),
            auth.uid(), auth.uid(), now());
    perform private.settle_order(o.id);
    v_first := false;
    v_paid := v_paid + 1;
  end loop;
  return jsonb_build_object('orders_paid', v_paid, 'amount', v_total, 'tip', v_tip, 'method', v_m);
end $$;

revoke all on function private.manual_method(text) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function public.record_manual_payment(uuid, text, text, numeric, numeric, text) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.settle_table_manual(uuid[], text, text, numeric, text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.record_manual_payment(uuid, text, text, numeric, numeric, text) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.settle_table_manual(uuid[], text, text, numeric, text) to authenticated;
````

<a id="45"></a>

## 45. 38-delivery.sql

`sql/38-delivery.sql` · 462 lines · md5 `0ec5b271d085da7281ebd47ec19b3e2b`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 38 · Delivery: zones, fee on the bill, dispatch and tracking
--
-- Zones   delivery_zones: an area (city or barangays) with its fee,
--         minimum order and usual travel time. The website and counter
--         list the active ones; the fee is added by the server.
-- Fee     orders.delivery_fee is part of the total (recompute below) and
--         can only change through set_delivery_fee (supervisor+).
-- Dispatch deliveries: one row per delivery order — our own rider or a
--         courier booked by hand (Lalamove / Grab / other) with the
--         tracking link and what the courier charged.
--         waiting → assigned → on_the_way → delivered (or failed).
-- Track   track_delivery(order_number, token) for the customer.
-- Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

select private.add_permission('delivery.dispatch', 'Orders', 'Dispatch deliveries', 'Mag-dispatch ng delivery',
  'Assign a rider or courier and move a delivery along.', 'dining', 145);
select private.add_permission('delivery.manage', 'Orders', 'Manage delivery zones', 'Pamahalaan ang delivery zone',
  'Set delivery areas, fees and minimum orders.', 'manager', 146);

create table if not exists public.delivery_zones (
  id           uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  branch_id    uuid not null references public.branches(id) on delete cascade,
  name         text not null check (length(btrim(name)) between 2 and 60),
  name_tl      text,
  areas        text,                                  -- barangays / landmarks, shown to the customer
  fee          numeric(10,2) not null default 0 check (fee >= 0 and fee <= 5000),
  min_order    numeric(10,2) not null default 0 check (min_order >= 0),
  eta_minutes  int check (eta_minutes is null or eta_minutes between 5 and 600),
  is_active    boolean not null default true,
  sort         int not null default 100,
  created_at   timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at   timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table public.delivery_zones enable row level security;
drop policy if exists delivery_zones_read on public.delivery_zones;
create policy delivery_zones_read on public.delivery_zones for select to anon, authenticated
  using (is_active or private.can('delivery.manage'));
drop policy if exists delivery_zones_write on public.delivery_zones;
create policy delivery_zones_write on public.delivery_zones for all to authenticated
  using (private.can('delivery.manage') and (branch_id = private.my_branch() or private.has_role('admin')))
  with check (private.can('delivery.manage') and (branch_id = private.my_branch() or private.has_role('admin')));
revoke all on public.delivery_zones from anon;
grant select on public.delivery_zones to anon;
grant select, insert, update, delete on public.delivery_zones to authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_delivery_zones_audit on public.delivery_zones;
create trigger trg_delivery_zones_audit after insert or update or delete on public.delivery_zones
  for each row execute function private.audit_row();

alter table public.orders add column if not exists delivery_zone_id uuid references public.delivery_zones(id) on delete set null;
alter table public.orders add column if not exists delivery_fee numeric(10,2) not null default 0;

-- The fee is part of the bill, so only the server writes it.
create or replace function private.trg_guard_delivery_fee()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if (new.delivery_fee is distinct from old.delivery_fee or new.delivery_zone_id is distinct from old.delivery_zone_id)
     and coalesce(current_setting('tpn.delivery', true), 'off') <> 'on' then
    raise exception 'delivery_fee_is_set_by_zone: use set_delivery_fee' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_guard_delivery_fee() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_guard_delivery_fee on public.orders;
create trigger trg_guard_delivery_fee before update on public.orders
  for each row execute function private.trg_guard_delivery_fee();

-- Totals now include the delivery fee.
create or replace function private.recompute_order_totals(p_order_id uuid)
returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  new_subtotal numeric(10,2);
  disc         numeric(10,2);
  svc          numeric(10,2);
  fee          numeric(10,2);
begin
  select coalesce(sum(total_price), 0) into new_subtotal
    from public.order_items
   where order_id = p_order_id and voided_at is null;

  select coalesce(discount_amount, 0), coalesce(service_charge, 0), coalesce(delivery_fee, 0)
    into disc, svc, fee
    from public.orders where id = p_order_id;

  perform set_config('tpn.recompute', 'on', true);
  update public.orders
     set subtotal = new_subtotal,
         total    = greatest(0, new_subtotal - coalesce(disc,0) + coalesce(svc,0) + coalesce(fee,0))
   where id = p_order_id;
  perform set_config('tpn.recompute', 'off', true);
end $$;
revoke execute on function private.recompute_order_totals(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;

create table if not exists public.deliveries (
  order_id      uuid primary key references public.orders(id) on delete cascade,
  branch_id     uuid not null references public.branches(id),
  status        text not null default 'waiting' check (status in ('waiting', 'assigned', 'on_the_way', 'delivered', 'failed')),
  courier       text check (courier in ('own', 'lalamove', 'grab', 'other')),
  rider_staff_id uuid references public.staff(id) on delete set null,
  rider_name    text,
  rider_phone   text,
  tracking_url  text check (tracking_url is null or tracking_url ~ '^https://'),
  courier_cost  numeric(10,2) check (courier_cost is null or courier_cost >= 0),
  note          text,
  fail_reason   text,
  assigned_at   timestamptz,
  picked_up_at  timestamptz,
  delivered_at  timestamptz,
  updated_by    uuid,
  created_at    timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at    timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index if not exists deliveries_branch_status_idx on public.deliveries (branch_id, status);
alter table public.deliveries enable row level security;
drop policy if exists deliveries_read on public.deliveries;
create policy deliveries_read on public.deliveries for select to authenticated
  using ((private.can('orders.view') or private.can('delivery.dispatch') or rider_staff_id = auth.uid())
         and (branch_id = private.my_branch() or private.has_role('admin')));
revoke all on public.deliveries from anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.deliveries to authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_deliveries_audit on public.deliveries;
create trigger trg_deliveries_audit after insert or update or delete on public.deliveries
  for each row execute function private.audit_row();

-- Existing open delivery orders get a dispatch row.
insert into public.deliveries (order_id, branch_id)
select o.id, o.branch_id from public.orders o
 where o.order_type = 'delivery' and o.status not in ('completed', 'cancelled')
on conflict (order_id) do nothing;

-- New signature (adds p_delivery_zone_id). Drop the old one so a call by
-- name is never ambiguous.
drop function if exists public.submit_order(uuid, text, jsonb, uuid, text, text, text, text, text, timestamptz, text, boolean, text);
create or replace function public.submit_order(
  p_branch_id uuid, p_order_type text, p_items jsonb,
  p_table_id uuid default null, p_customer_name text default null,
  p_customer_phone text default null, p_notes text default null,
  p_payment_method text default null, p_delivery_address text default null,
  p_scheduled_for timestamptz default null, p_device_id text default null,
  p_join_table_bill boolean default true, p_source text default null,
  p_delivery_zone_id uuid default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_order_id uuid; v_number text; v_token uuid; v_item jsonb;
  v_appended boolean := false; v_qty int; v_price numeric; v_batch numeric := 0;
  v_flagged boolean := false; v_mi public.menu_items%rowtype; v_pax text;
  v_clean jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; v_mods jsonb; v_mod_total numeric; v_g record;
  v_source text;
  v_zone public.delivery_zones%rowtype; v_fee numeric(10,2) := 0;
  v_device   text := private.clean_device_id(p_device_id);
  v_name     text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_customer_name, '')), ''), 60);
  v_phone    text := left(nullif(regexp_replace(coalesce(p_customer_phone, ''), '[^0-9+]', '', 'g'), ''), 16);
  v_notes    text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_notes, '')), ''), 500);
  v_address  text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_delivery_address, '')), ''), 300);
  v_staff    boolean := private.my_role() is not null;
begin
  -- A signed-in staff member ringing up at the counter is not rate limited;
  -- an anonymous caller is.
  if not v_staff then
    perform private.rate_limit('submit_order', 20, interval '10 minutes');
  elsif not (private.can('pos.use') or private.can('orders.manage')) then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: pos.use' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  v_source := case when p_source in ('web', 'qr', 'pos', 'phone') then p_source
                   when v_staff then 'pos' when p_table_id is not null then 'qr' else 'web' end;
  if not v_staff and v_source in ('pos', 'phone') then v_source := 'web'; end if;

  if p_items is null or jsonb_typeof(p_items) <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(p_items) = 0 then
    raise exception 'no_items: an order needs at least one line' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if jsonb_array_length(p_items) > 40 then
    raise exception 'too_many_lines: split this into two orders' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_order_type not in ('dine_in', 'pickup', 'delivery') then
    raise exception 'bad_order_type: %', p_order_type using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_order_type = 'delivery' and v_address is null then
    raise exception 'delivery_needs_address' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.branches b where b.id = p_branch_id and b.is_active) then
    raise exception 'branch_not_found' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_staff and p_branch_id is distinct from private.my_branch() and not private.has_role('admin') then
    raise exception 'wrong_branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_table_id is not null and not exists (
       select 1 from public.restaurant_tables t
        where t.id = p_table_id and t.branch_id = p_branch_id and t.is_active) then
    raise exception 'table_not_in_branch' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_scheduled_for is not null
     and (p_scheduled_for < now() - interval '5 minutes' or p_scheduled_for > now() + interval '60 days') then
    raise exception 'bad_schedule_time' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  for v_item in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_items) loop
    begin
      select * into v_mi from public.menu_items where id = nullif(v_item->>'menu_item_id', '')::uuid;
    exception when invalid_text_representation then
      raise exception 'unknown_item' using errcode = '22023';
    end;
    if not found then raise exception 'unknown_item' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    if not coalesce(v_mi.is_available, true) then
      raise exception 'item_sold_out: %', v_mi.name using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    v_qty := coalesce((v_item->>'quantity')::int, 1);
    if v_qty < 1 or v_qty > 50 then
      raise exception 'bad_quantity: % (1 to 50 per line)', v_mi.name using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    -- Size as in sql/29c: "2-3" or "2-3 pax"; listed size → its price,
    -- no size → the menu price.
    v_pax := nullif(regexp_replace(btrim(coalesce(v_item->>'pax_size', '')), '\s*pax$', '', 'i'), '');
    if v_pax is not null then
      if v_mi.pax_options is null or jsonb_typeof(v_mi.pax_options) <> 'object' or not (v_mi.pax_options ? v_pax) then
        raise exception 'unknown_size: % (%)', v_mi.name, v_pax using errcode = '22023';
      end if;
      v_price := (v_mi.pax_options->>v_pax)::numeric;
    else
      v_price := v_mi.price;
    end if;
    if v_price is null or v_price < 0 then
      raise exception 'item_has_no_price: %', v_mi.name using errcode = '22023';
    end if;

    -- Modifiers: only ones linked to this dish, available, within each
    -- group's min/max. Priced here.
    v_mods := '[]'::jsonb; v_mod_total := 0;
    if jsonb_typeof(v_item->'modifiers') = 'array' and jsonb_array_length(v_item->'modifiers') > 0 then
      if jsonb_array_length(v_item->'modifiers') > 20 then raise exception 'too_many_modifiers' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', m.id, 'group', g.name, 'name', m.name, 'price', m.price_delta)
                                order by g.sort, m.sort), '[]'::jsonb),
             coalesce(sum(m.price_delta), 0)
        into v_mods, v_mod_total
        from public.modifiers m
        join public.modifier_groups g on g.id = m.group_id and g.is_active
        join public.menu_item_modifier_groups l on l.group_id = g.id and l.menu_item_id = v_mi.id
       where m.is_available
         and m.id in (select (x #>> '{}')::uuid from jsonb_array_elements(v_item->'modifiers') x);
      if jsonb_array_length(v_mods) <> (select count(distinct x #>> '{}') from jsonb_array_elements(v_item->'modifiers') x) then
        raise exception 'unknown_modifier: %', v_mi.name using errcode = '22023';
      end if;
    end if;
    for v_g in
      select g.id, g.name, g.min_select, g.max_select,
             (select count(*) from jsonb_array_elements(v_mods) s
               join public.modifiers m2 on m2.id = (s->>'id')::uuid where m2.group_id = g.id) as picked
        from public.modifier_groups g
        join public.menu_item_modifier_groups l on l.group_id = g.id and l.menu_item_id = v_mi.id
       where g.is_active
    loop
      if v_g.picked < v_g.min_select or v_g.picked > v_g.max_select then
        raise exception 'modifier_choice: % needs % to % from "%"', v_mi.name, v_g.min_select, v_g.max_select, v_g.name using errcode = '22023';
      end if;
    end loop;
    v_price := v_price + v_mod_total;
    if v_price < 0 then v_price := 0; end if;

    v_clean := v_clean || jsonb_build_object(
      'menu_item_id', v_mi.id, 'name', v_mi.name, 'unit_price', v_price, 'quantity', v_qty,
      'pax_size', v_pax, 'modifiers', v_mods,
      'notes', left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(v_item->>'notes', '')), ''), 200));
  end loop;

  -- Delivery: when the branch has zones, one must be picked; its fee goes on
  -- the bill and its minimum order applies (sql/38).
  if p_order_type = 'delivery'
     and exists (select 1 from public.delivery_zones z where z.branch_id = p_branch_id and z.is_active) then
    if p_delivery_zone_id is null then raise exception 'delivery_needs_zone' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    select * into v_zone from public.delivery_zones z
     where z.id = p_delivery_zone_id and z.branch_id = p_branch_id and z.is_active;
    if not found then raise exception 'delivery_zone_unknown' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    if v_zone.min_order > 0 and (select coalesce(sum((x->>'unit_price')::numeric * (x->>'quantity')::int), 0)
                                   from jsonb_array_elements(v_clean) x) < v_zone.min_order then
      raise exception 'delivery_min_order: %', v_zone.min_order using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    v_fee := v_zone.fee;
  end if;

  if p_order_type = 'dine_in' and p_table_id is not null and p_join_table_bill then
    select id into v_order_id from public.orders
     where table_id = p_table_id and order_type = 'dine_in'
       and status not in ('completed', 'cancelled') and payment_status <> 'paid'
     order by placed_at limit 1 for update;
    v_appended := v_order_id is not null;
  end if;

  if v_order_id is null then
    insert into public.orders (branch_id, table_id, order_type, customer_name, customer_phone,
      subtotal, total, payment_method, notes, delivery_address, scheduled_for, source,
      delivery_zone_id, delivery_fee)
    values (p_branch_id, p_table_id, p_order_type::public.order_type_enum, v_name, v_phone, 0, 0,
      nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_payment_method, '')), '')::public.payment_method,
      v_notes, v_address, p_scheduled_for, v_source, v_zone.id, v_fee)
    returning id into v_order_id;
    if p_order_type = 'delivery' then
      insert into public.deliveries (order_id, branch_id) values (v_order_id, p_branch_id) on conflict (order_id) do nothing;
    end if;
  elsif v_notes is not null then
    update public.orders set notes = left(concat_ws(E'\n', nullif(notes, ''), v_notes), 2000) where id = v_order_id;
  end if;

  for v_item in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_clean) loop
    v_qty := (v_item->>'quantity')::int;
    v_price := (v_item->>'unit_price')::numeric;
    v_batch := v_batch + (v_price * v_qty);
    insert into public.order_items (order_id, menu_item_id, name_snapshot, price_snapshot,
      quantity, pax_size, unit_price, total_price, notes, guest_name, device_id, modifiers)
    values (v_order_id, (v_item->>'menu_item_id')::uuid, v_item->>'name', v_price,
      v_qty, v_item->>'pax_size', v_price, v_price * v_qty, v_item->>'notes', v_name, v_device,
      coalesce(v_item->'modifiers', '[]'::jsonb));
  end loop;

  if not v_staff then
    perform private.remember_device(v_device, p_branch_id, v_name, v_phone, v_batch, v_clean);
  end if;

  select order_number, track_token into v_number, v_token from public.orders where id = v_order_id;
  select is_flagged into v_flagged from public.customer_devices where device_id = v_device;

  return jsonb_build_object(
    'id', v_order_id, 'order_number', v_number, 'track_token', v_token,
    'joined_existing', v_appended, 'device_flagged', coalesce(v_flagged, false),
    'total',    (select total    from public.orders where id = v_order_id),
    'subtotal', (select subtotal from public.orders where id = v_order_id),
    'delivery_fee', v_fee);
end $$;
revoke all on function public.submit_order(uuid, text, jsonb, uuid, text, text, text, text, text, timestamptz, text, boolean, text, uuid) from public;
grant execute on function public.submit_order(uuid, text, jsonb, uuid, text, text, text, text, text, timestamptz, text, boolean, text, uuid) to anon, authenticated;

-- Change a delivery order's zone (or waive the fee) after the fact.
create or replace function public.set_delivery_fee(p_order_id uuid, p_zone_id uuid default null, p_fee numeric default null, p_reason text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare o public.orders%rowtype; z public.delivery_zones%rowtype; v_fee numeric(10,2);
begin
  if not private.has_role('supervisor') then raise exception 'permission_denied: supervisor' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into o from public.orders where id = p_order_id for update;
  if not found or o.order_type <> 'delivery' then raise exception 'not_a_delivery'; end if;
  perform private.assert_order_access(o);
  if o.payment_status = 'paid' then raise exception 'order_already_paid'; end if;
  if p_zone_id is not null then
    select * into z from public.delivery_zones where id = p_zone_id and branch_id = o.branch_id;
    if not found then raise exception 'delivery_zone_unknown'; end if;
  end if;
  v_fee := round(coalesce(p_fee, z.fee, o.delivery_fee), 2);
  if v_fee < 0 or v_fee > 5000 then raise exception 'bad_amount'; end if;
  if p_fee is not null and length(btrim(coalesce(p_reason, ''))) < 3 then raise exception 'reason_required'; end if;
  perform set_config('tpn.delivery', 'on', true);
  update public.orders set delivery_fee = v_fee, delivery_zone_id = coalesce(p_zone_id, delivery_zone_id) where id = o.id;
  perform set_config('tpn.delivery', 'off', true);
  perform private.recompute_order_totals(o.id);
  insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
  values (auth.uid(), private.my_role(), 'order.delivery_fee', 'order', o.id,
          jsonb_build_object('before', o.delivery_fee, 'after', v_fee, 'zone', p_zone_id, 'reason', p_reason));
  return jsonb_build_object('delivery_fee', v_fee, 'total', (select total from public.orders where id = o.id));
end $$;

-- Hand a delivery to our rider or a courier.
create or replace function public.delivery_assign(
  p_order_id uuid, p_courier text, p_rider_staff_id uuid default null, p_rider_name text default null,
  p_rider_phone text default null, p_tracking_url text default null, p_courier_cost numeric default null, p_note text default null)
returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare o public.orders%rowtype; v_name text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_rider_name, '')), ''), 60);
begin
  if not private.can('delivery.dispatch') then raise exception 'permission_denied: delivery.dispatch' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_courier not in ('own', 'lalamove', 'grab', 'other') then raise exception 'bad_courier'; end if;
  if p_tracking_url is not null and btrim(p_tracking_url) <> '' and p_tracking_url !~ '^https://' then raise exception 'bad_tracking_url'; end if;
  select * into o from public.orders where id = p_order_id;
  if not found or o.order_type <> 'delivery' then raise exception 'not_a_delivery'; end if;
  perform private.assert_order_access(o);
  if o.status in ('completed', 'cancelled') then raise exception 'order_closed'; end if;
  if p_courier = 'own' and p_rider_staff_id is not null then
    select coalesce(v_name, full_name) into v_name from public.staff where id = p_rider_staff_id;
  end if;
  insert into public.deliveries (order_id, branch_id) values (o.id, o.branch_id) on conflict (order_id) do nothing;
  update public.deliveries
     set status = case when status in ('waiting', 'failed') then 'assigned' else status end,
         courier = p_courier, rider_staff_id = case when p_courier = 'own' then p_rider_staff_id end,
         rider_name = v_name, rider_phone = left(nullif(regexp_replace(coalesce(p_rider_phone, ''), '[^0-9+]', '', 'g'), ''), 16),
         tracking_url = nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_tracking_url, '')), ''),
         courier_cost = case when p_courier_cost is null then null else round(p_courier_cost, 2) end,
         note = left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_note, '')), ''), 300), fail_reason = null,
         assigned_at = coalesce(assigned_at, now()), updated_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()
   where order_id = o.id;
end $$;

-- Move it along: on_the_way / delivered / failed / waiting.
-- The rider assigned to it may update it too.
create or replace function public.delivery_update(p_order_id uuid, p_status text, p_reason text default null)
returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare o public.orders%rowtype; d public.deliveries%rowtype;
begin
  if p_status not in ('waiting', 'on_the_way', 'delivered', 'failed') then raise exception 'bad_status'; end if;
  select * into o from public.orders where id = p_order_id for update;
  if not found or o.order_type <> 'delivery' then raise exception 'not_a_delivery'; end if;
  perform private.assert_order_access(o);
  select * into d from public.deliveries where order_id = o.id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'not_dispatched'; end if;
  if not (private.can('delivery.dispatch') or d.rider_staff_id = auth.uid()) then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: delivery.dispatch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_status = 'failed' and length(btrim(coalesce(p_reason, ''))) < 3 then raise exception 'reason_required'; end if;
  if p_status in ('on_the_way', 'delivered') and d.courier is null then raise exception 'assign_first'; end if;

  update public.deliveries
     set status = p_status,
         picked_up_at = case when p_status = 'on_the_way' then coalesce(picked_up_at, now()) else picked_up_at end,
         delivered_at = case when p_status = 'delivered' then now() else delivered_at end,
         fail_reason = case when p_status = 'failed' then left(btrim(p_reason), 300) end,
         updated_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()
   where order_id = o.id;

  -- The order itself follows: on the way = served, delivered = completed.
  if p_status = 'on_the_way' and o.status in ('pending', 'confirmed', 'preparing', 'ready') then
    update public.orders set status = 'served', served_at = coalesce(served_at, now()) where id = o.id;
  elsif p_status = 'delivered' and o.status not in ('completed', 'cancelled') then
    update public.orders set status = 'completed', completed_at = now(),
           served_at = coalesce(served_at, now()) where id = o.id;
  end if;
end $$;

-- What the customer sees on their tracking page.
create or replace function public.track_delivery(p_order_number text, p_token uuid)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare o public.orders%rowtype; d public.deliveries%rowtype; z public.delivery_zones%rowtype;
begin
  perform private.rate_limit('track_delivery', 60, interval '10 minutes');
  select * into o from public.orders where order_number = left(p_order_number, 40) and track_token = p_token;
  if not found or o.order_type <> 'delivery' then return null; end if;
  select * into d from public.deliveries where order_id = o.id;
  select * into z from public.delivery_zones where id = o.delivery_zone_id;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'status', coalesce(d.status, 'waiting'),
    'courier', d.courier,
    'rider', split_part(coalesce(d.rider_name, ''), ' ', 1),
    'tracking_url', d.tracking_url,
    'zone', z.name, 'eta_minutes', z.eta_minutes, 'fee', o.delivery_fee,
    'on_the_way_at', d.picked_up_at, 'delivered_at', d.delivered_at);
end $$;

revoke all on function public.set_delivery_fee(uuid, uuid, numeric, text) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.delivery_assign(uuid, text, uuid, text, text, text, numeric, text) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.delivery_update(uuid, text, text) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.track_delivery(text, uuid) from public;
grant execute on function public.set_delivery_fee(uuid, uuid, numeric, text) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.delivery_assign(uuid, text, uuid, text, text, text, numeric, text) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.delivery_update(uuid, text, text) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.track_delivery(text, uuid) to anon, authenticated;
````

## 46. 39-menu-oct-2026.sql

`sql/39-menu-oct-2026.sql` · 149 lines · md5 `90e60b5516908256d7155837a8dfaa02`

````sql
-- 39 · The real menu (October 2026), typed from the printed menu boards.
-- Data only. Safe to re-run: dishes are matched by name and updated.
--   2-3 / 4-6 pax columns → pax_options {"2-3": x, "4-6": y}
--   one price + a 4-6 pax column (rice, soda, juice) → {"1": x, "4-6": y}
--   same price in both columns (nachos, fish & fries, calamares) → one price
--   ★ on the board → is_featured, tag "Bestseller"
-- Dishes no longer on the boards are hidden (or deleted if never ordered).

do $$
declare
  v_branches jsonb := '{"tgt": true, "bacoor": true, "laspinas": true}';
  r record; v_cat uuid; v_id uuid;
begin
  -- Categories: reuse the old rows where they fit, so links stay intact.
  update public.menu_categories set name = 'All-Day Breakfast Silog' where name = 'Silog Meals';
  update public.menu_categories set name = 'Vegetable Dishes' where name = 'Vegetables & Sides';
  update public.menu_categories set name = 'Merienda & Pica-Pica' where name = 'Snacks & Merienda';
  update public.menu_categories set name = 'Drinks' where name = 'Beverages';
  update public.menu_categories set name = 'Additional' where name = 'Rice Bowls';

  for r in select * from (values
    ('All-Day Breakfast Silog', 'Silog (buong araw)', 1, '🍳'),
    ('Pork Dishes',             'Baboy',              2, '🐖'),
    ('Chicken Dishes',          'Manok',              3, '🍗'),
    ('Beef Dishes',             'Baka',               4, '🐄'),
    ('Seafood Dishes',          'Pagkaing-dagat',     5, '🦐'),
    ('Vegetable Dishes',        'Gulay',              6, '🥬'),
    ('Wings & Thigh Fillet',    'Wings at Thigh Fillet', 7, '🍗'),
    ('Merienda & Pica-Pica',    'Meryenda at Pica-Pica', 8, '🍜'),
    ('Additional',              'Dagdag',             9, '🍚'),
    ('Drinks',                  'Inumin',            10, '🥤')
  ) as c(name, tl, ord, icon) loop
    update public.menu_categories set name_tagalog = r.tl, display_order = r.ord, icon = r.icon, is_active = true
     where name = r.name;
    if not found then
      insert into public.menu_categories (name, name_tagalog, display_order, icon, is_active)
      values (r.name, r.tl, r.ord, r.icon, true);
    end if;
  end loop;
  update public.menu_categories set is_active = false
   where name not in ('All-Day Breakfast Silog','Pork Dishes','Chicken Dishes','Beef Dishes','Seafood Dishes',
                      'Vegetable Dishes','Wings & Thigh Fillet','Merienda & Pica-Pica','Additional','Drinks');

  -- Old names that are the same dish under a new name.
  update public.menu_items set name = 'Cornedbeef Silog' where name = 'Cornsilog';
  update public.menu_items set name = 'Chicksilog' where name = 'Chicken Silog'
     and not exists (select 1 from public.menu_items where name = 'Chicksilog');
  update public.menu_items set name = 'Pork Sinigang' where name = 'Sinigang na Baboy';

  create temp table _menu (cat text, name text, price numeric, pax jsonb, star boolean, emoji text, descr text, ord int) on commit drop;
  insert into _menu values
    -- All-day breakfast silog (single plate)
    ('All-Day Breakfast Silog','Tapsilog',          145, null, true,  '🍳', 'Beef tapa, garlic rice and egg', 1),
    ('All-Day Breakfast Silog','Bacon Silog',       145, null, true,  '🥓', 'Bacon, garlic rice and egg', 2),

    ('Merienda & Pica-Pica','Pancit Sotanghon',   275, '{"2-3":275,"4-6":490}', true,  '🍜', null, 3),
    ('Merienda & Pica-Pica','Pancit Malabon',     265, '{"2-3":265,"4-6":460}', true,  '🍜', null, 4),
    ('Merienda & Pica-Pica','Pancit Malabon with Lechon Kawali', 365, '{"2-3":365,"4-6":580}', true, '🍜', null, 5),
    ('Merienda & Pica-Pica','Nachos',             245, null, false, '🧀', 'Regular, chicken or sisig', 6),
    ('Merienda & Pica-Pica','Fish & Fries',       265, null, false, '🐟', null, 7),
    ('Merienda & Pica-Pica','Calamares',          265, null, true,  '🦑', null, 8),
    -- Additional
    ('Additional','Plain Rice',   35, '{"1":35,"4-6":200}', false, '🍚', null, 1),
    ('Additional','Garlic Rice',  40, '{"1":40,"4-6":230}', false, '🍚', null, 2),
    ('Additional','Extra Egg',    25, null, false, '🍳', 'Sunny side up, scrambled or boiled', 3),
    ('Additional','Mayo Dip',     25, null, false, '🥣', null, 4),
    ('Additional','Bagoong',      20, null, false, '🥣', null, 5),
    ('Additional','Unli Rice',    29, null, false, '🍚', 'Add to a silog meal. Dine-in only.', 6),
    -- Drinks
    ('Drinks','Brewed Coffee',  90, null, false, '☕', 'Hot or iced', 1),
    ('Drinks','Soda in Can',    65, '{"1":65,"4-6":120}', false, '🥤', 'Coke Regular, Coke Zero, Royal or Sprite', 2),
    ('Drinks','Bottled Water',  30, null, false, '💧', null, 3),
    ('Drinks','Juice Drink',    70, '{"1":70,"4-6":135}', false, '🧃', null, 4);

  for r in select m.*, c.id cat_id from _menu m join public.menu_categories c on c.name = m.cat loop
    select id into v_id from public.menu_items where lower(name) = lower(r.name) limit 1;
    if v_id is null then
      insert into public.menu_items (category_id, name, price, pax_options, is_shareable, is_available,
                                     is_featured, featured_tag, emoji, description, display_order, branch_availability)
      values (r.cat_id, r.name, r.price, r.pax, r.pax is not null, true,
              r.star, case when r.star then 'Bestseller' end, r.emoji, r.descr, r.ord, v_branches);
    else
      update public.menu_items set category_id = r.cat_id, price = r.price, pax_options = r.pax,
             is_shareable = r.pax is not null, is_available = true, is_featured = r.star,
             featured_tag = case when r.star then 'Bestseller' end, emoji = r.emoji,
             description = coalesce(r.descr, description), display_order = r.ord,
             branch_availability = v_branches, updated_at = now()
       where id = v_id;
    end if;
  end loop;

  -- Off the boards: delete if never ordered, otherwise hide.
  delete from public.menu_items mi
   where not exists (select 1 from _menu m where lower(m.name) = lower(mi.name))
     and not exists (select 1 from public.order_items oi where oi.menu_item_id = mi.id)
     and not exists (select 1 from public.customer_device_items d where d.menu_item_id = mi.id);
  update public.menu_items mi set is_available = false, is_featured = false
   where not exists (select 1 from _menu m where lower(m.name) = lower(mi.name));
end $$;
````

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 40 · Short descriptions for the dishes that had none
-- Data only. Safe to re-run: fills a description only where it is empty,
-- so anything typed in the portal's Menu Manager is never overwritten.
-- The Tagalog for each line is in tpn-i18n.js (PHRASES), which swaps the
-- text on screen; there is no Tagalog description column.
-- Wording is generic on purpose: correct any dish in Menu Manager.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
update public.menu_items m
   set description = v.d
  from (values
  ('Adobong Pusit with Gata', 'Squid stewed in vinegar and soy, finished in coconut milk'),
  ('Bagoong', 'Sautéed shrimp paste on the side'),

  ('Buffalo Thigh Fillet', 'Crispy chicken thigh fillet in buffalo sauce'),

  ('Buttered Shrimp', 'Shrimp sautéed in garlic butter'),
  ('Calamares', 'Crispy fried squid rings with dip'),
  ('Chicken Adobo with Gata', 'Chicken adobo finished in coconut milk'),
  ('Chicken Bistek', 'Chicken in soy sauce and calamansi with onion rings'),
  ('Chicken Curry', 'Chicken, potatoes and carrots in curry sauce'),
  ('Fish & Fries', 'Breaded fish fillet with fries'),
  ('Garlic Parmesan Thigh Fillet', 'Crispy chicken thigh fillet in garlic parmesan'),
  ('Garlic Parmesan Wings', 'Chicken wings tossed in garlic parmesan'),
  ('Garlic Rice', 'Rice fried with toasted garlic'),
  ('Ginataang Hipon', 'Shrimp in coconut milk'),
  ('Ginataang Sitaw at Kalabasa', 'String beans and squash in coconut milk'),
  ('Juice Drink', 'Chilled juice drink'),
  ('Kalderetang Bulacan', 'Bulacan-style chicken caldereta in tomato sauce'),
  ('Lechon Kawali', 'Crispy deep-fried pork belly'),
  ('Mayo Dip', 'Creamy mayo dip'),
  ('Mixed Vegetables', 'Sautéed mixed vegetables'),
  ('Pancit Bihon', 'Rice noodles stir-fried with vegetables'),
  ('Pancit Canton', 'Egg noodles stir-fried with vegetables'),
  ('Pancit Malabon', 'Thick rice noodles in shrimp sauce with toppings'),
  ('Pancit Malabon with Lechon Kawali', 'Pancit Malabon topped with crispy lechon kawali'),
  ('Pancit Sotanghon', 'Glass noodles stir-fried with vegetables'),
  ('Plain Rice', 'Steamed white rice'),

  ('Sinigang na Hipon', 'Shrimp in tamarind-based sour broth with vegetables'),
  ('Sinigang na Salmon', 'Salmon in tamarind-based sour broth with vegetables'),
  ('Teriyaki Wings', 'Chicken wings glazed in teriyaki with sesame')
  ) as v(n, d)
 where m.name = v.n
   and coalesce(btrim(m.description), '') = '';
````

<a id="48"></a>

## 48. 41-order-idempotency.sql

`sql/41-order-idempotency.sql` · 280 lines · md5 `3ee4ded777082d03172ace37126794ef`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 41 · Order idempotency: a retry can't place a second order
--
-- Problem  The connection drops after submit_order() saved the order but
--          before the reply arrives. The customer taps Place Order again
--          and a second order is created.
-- Key      The browser makes one random key per checkout attempt
--          (crypto.randomUUID) and sends it as p_client_key. It is kept
--          in the tab's checkout state, so a refresh reuses it.
-- Stored   orders.client_key (unique) for an order this call opened, and
--          order_items.client_key on every line it added, which also
--          covers a round appended to a table's open bill.
-- Repeat   A call with a key already used in this branch inserts nothing
--          and returns the original order in the same jsonb shape.
-- Rules    Server pricing, the rate limit and the grants are unchanged.
--          The key is checked after the rate limit and the branch checks.
-- Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

alter table public.orders      add column if not exists client_key text;
alter table public.order_items add column if not exists client_key text;
create unique index if not exists orders_client_key_uniq on public.orders (client_key) where client_key is not null;
create index if not exists order_items_client_key_idx on public.order_items (client_key) where client_key is not null;

-- The reply submit_order() gives, rebuilt for an order that already exists.
create or replace function private.submit_order_reply(p_order_id uuid, p_appended boolean, p_device text)
returns jsonb
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'id', o.id, 'order_number', o.order_number, 'track_token', o.track_token,
    'joined_existing', p_appended,
    'device_flagged', coalesce((select d.is_flagged from public.customer_devices d where d.device_id = p_device), false),
    'total', o.total, 'subtotal', o.subtotal,
    'delivery_fee', case when p_appended then 0 else coalesce(o.delivery_fee, 0) end)
    from public.orders o where o.id = p_order_id
$$;
revoke all on function private.submit_order_reply(uuid, boolean, text) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- New signature (adds p_client_key). Drop the old one so a call by name is
-- never ambiguous.
drop function if exists public.submit_order(uuid, text, jsonb, uuid, text, text, text, text, text, timestamptz, text, boolean, text, uuid);
create or replace function public.submit_order(
  p_branch_id uuid, p_order_type text, p_items jsonb,
  p_table_id uuid default null, p_customer_name text default null,
  p_customer_phone text default null, p_notes text default null,
  p_payment_method text default null, p_delivery_address text default null,
  p_scheduled_for timestamptz default null, p_device_id text default null,
  p_join_table_bill boolean default true, p_source text default null,
  p_delivery_zone_id uuid default null, p_client_key text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_order_id uuid; v_number text; v_token uuid; v_item jsonb;
  v_appended boolean := false; v_qty int; v_price numeric; v_batch numeric := 0;
  v_flagged boolean := false; v_mi public.menu_items%rowtype; v_pax text;
  v_clean jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; v_mods jsonb; v_mod_total numeric; v_g record;
  v_source text;
  v_zone public.delivery_zones%rowtype; v_fee numeric(10,2) := 0;
  v_device   text := private.clean_device_id(p_device_id);
  v_name     text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_customer_name, '')), ''), 60);
  v_phone    text := left(nullif(regexp_replace(coalesce(p_customer_phone, ''), '[^0-9+]', '', 'g'), ''), 16);
  v_notes    text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_notes, '')), ''), 500);
  v_address  text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_delivery_address, '')), ''), 300);
  v_staff    boolean := private.my_role() is not null;
  v_key      text := nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_client_key, '')), '');
  v_found    uuid; v_con text;
begin
  -- A signed-in staff member ringing up at the counter is not rate limited;
  -- an anonymous caller is.
  if not v_staff then
    perform private.rate_limit('submit_order', 20, interval '10 minutes');
  elsif not (private.can('pos.use') or private.can('orders.manage')) then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: pos.use' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  v_source := case when p_source in ('web', 'qr', 'pos', 'phone') then p_source
                   when v_staff then 'pos' when p_table_id is not null then 'qr' else 'web' end;
  if not v_staff and v_source in ('pos', 'phone') then v_source := 'web'; end if;

  if p_items is null or jsonb_typeof(p_items) <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(p_items) = 0 then
    raise exception 'no_items: an order needs at least one line' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if jsonb_array_length(p_items) > 40 then
    raise exception 'too_many_lines: split this into two orders' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_order_type not in ('dine_in', 'pickup', 'delivery') then
    raise exception 'bad_order_type: %', p_order_type using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_order_type = 'delivery' and v_address is null then
    raise exception 'delivery_needs_address' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.branches b where b.id = p_branch_id and b.is_active) then
    raise exception 'branch_not_found' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_staff and p_branch_id is distinct from private.my_branch() and not private.has_role('admin') then
    raise exception 'wrong_branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  -- Idempotency (sql/41): a key this branch has seen means this is a retry.
  -- Answer with the original order before validating anything else, so a
  -- dish that sold out in between can't turn a retry into an error.
  if v_key is not null then
    if v_key !~ '^[A-Za-z0-9-]{16,64}$' then
      raise exception 'bad_client_key' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    select o.id into v_found from public.orders o where o.client_key = v_key and o.branch_id = p_branch_id;
    if v_found is not null then return private.submit_order_reply(v_found, false, v_device); end if;
    select oi.order_id into v_found from public.order_items oi
      join public.orders o on o.id = oi.order_id and o.branch_id = p_branch_id
     where oi.client_key = v_key limit 1;
    if v_found is not null then return private.submit_order_reply(v_found, true, v_device); end if;
  end if;
  if p_table_id is not null and not exists (
       select 1 from public.restaurant_tables t
        where t.id = p_table_id and t.branch_id = p_branch_id and t.is_active) then
    raise exception 'table_not_in_branch' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_scheduled_for is not null
     and (p_scheduled_for < now() - interval '5 minutes' or p_scheduled_for > now() + interval '60 days') then
    raise exception 'bad_schedule_time' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  for v_item in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_items) loop
    begin
      select * into v_mi from public.menu_items where id = nullif(v_item->>'menu_item_id', '')::uuid;
    exception when invalid_text_representation then
      raise exception 'unknown_item' using errcode = '22023';
    end;
    if not found then raise exception 'unknown_item' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    if not coalesce(v_mi.is_available, true) then
      raise exception 'item_sold_out: %', v_mi.name using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    v_qty := coalesce((v_item->>'quantity')::int, 1);
    if v_qty < 1 or v_qty > 50 then
      raise exception 'bad_quantity: % (1 to 50 per line)', v_mi.name using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    -- Size as in sql/29c: "2-3" or "2-3 pax"; listed size → its price,
    -- no size → the menu price.
    v_pax := nullif(regexp_replace(btrim(coalesce(v_item->>'pax_size', '')), '\s*pax$', '', 'i'), '');
    if v_pax is not null then
      if v_mi.pax_options is null or jsonb_typeof(v_mi.pax_options) <> 'object' or not (v_mi.pax_options ? v_pax) then
        raise exception 'unknown_size: % (%)', v_mi.name, v_pax using errcode = '22023';
      end if;
      v_price := (v_mi.pax_options->>v_pax)::numeric;
    else
      v_price := v_mi.price;
    end if;
    if v_price is null or v_price < 0 then
      raise exception 'item_has_no_price: %', v_mi.name using errcode = '22023';
    end if;

    -- Modifiers: only ones linked to this dish, available, within each
    -- group's min/max. Priced here.
    v_mods := '[]'::jsonb; v_mod_total := 0;
    if jsonb_typeof(v_item->'modifiers') = 'array' and jsonb_array_length(v_item->'modifiers') > 0 then
      if jsonb_array_length(v_item->'modifiers') > 20 then raise exception 'too_many_modifiers' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', m.id, 'group', g.name, 'name', m.name, 'price', m.price_delta)
                                order by g.sort, m.sort), '[]'::jsonb),
             coalesce(sum(m.price_delta), 0)
        into v_mods, v_mod_total
        from public.modifiers m
        join public.modifier_groups g on g.id = m.group_id and g.is_active
        join public.menu_item_modifier_groups l on l.group_id = g.id and l.menu_item_id = v_mi.id
       where m.is_available
         and m.id in (select (x #>> '{}')::uuid from jsonb_array_elements(v_item->'modifiers') x);
      if jsonb_array_length(v_mods) <> (select count(distinct x #>> '{}') from jsonb_array_elements(v_item->'modifiers') x) then
        raise exception 'unknown_modifier: %', v_mi.name using errcode = '22023';
      end if;
    end if;
    for v_g in
      select g.id, g.name, g.min_select, g.max_select,
             (select count(*) from jsonb_array_elements(v_mods) s
               join public.modifiers m2 on m2.id = (s->>'id')::uuid where m2.group_id = g.id) as picked
        from public.modifier_groups g
        join public.menu_item_modifier_groups l on l.group_id = g.id and l.menu_item_id = v_mi.id
       where g.is_active
    loop
      if v_g.picked < v_g.min_select or v_g.picked > v_g.max_select then
        raise exception 'modifier_choice: % needs % to % from "%"', v_mi.name, v_g.min_select, v_g.max_select, v_g.name using errcode = '22023';
      end if;
    end loop;
    v_price := v_price + v_mod_total;
    if v_price < 0 then v_price := 0; end if;

    v_clean := v_clean || jsonb_build_object(
      'menu_item_id', v_mi.id, 'name', v_mi.name, 'unit_price', v_price, 'quantity', v_qty,
      'pax_size', v_pax, 'modifiers', v_mods,
      'notes', left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(v_item->>'notes', '')), ''), 200));
  end loop;

  -- Delivery: when the branch has zones, one must be picked; its fee goes on
  -- the bill and its minimum order applies (sql/38).
  if p_order_type = 'delivery'
     and exists (select 1 from public.delivery_zones z where z.branch_id = p_branch_id and z.is_active) then
    if p_delivery_zone_id is null then raise exception 'delivery_needs_zone' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    select * into v_zone from public.delivery_zones z
     where z.id = p_delivery_zone_id and z.branch_id = p_branch_id and z.is_active;
    if not found then raise exception 'delivery_zone_unknown' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    if v_zone.min_order > 0 and (select coalesce(sum((x->>'unit_price')::numeric * (x->>'quantity')::int), 0)
                                   from jsonb_array_elements(v_clean) x) < v_zone.min_order then
      raise exception 'delivery_min_order: %', v_zone.min_order using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    v_fee := v_zone.fee;
  end if;

  if p_order_type = 'dine_in' and p_table_id is not null and p_join_table_bill then
    select id into v_order_id from public.orders
     where table_id = p_table_id and order_type = 'dine_in'
       and status not in ('completed', 'cancelled') and payment_status <> 'paid'
     order by placed_at limit 1 for update;
    v_appended := v_order_id is not null;
    -- Two copies of one round racing: the second waits on the lock above,
    -- then sees the first one's lines here.
    if v_appended and v_key is not null
       and exists (select 1 from public.order_items oi where oi.order_id = v_order_id and oi.client_key = v_key) then
      return private.submit_order_reply(v_order_id, true, v_device);
    end if;
  end if;

  if v_order_id is null then
    -- Two copies of one order racing: the unique index makes the second
    -- wait for the first, then fail here, and it answers with the first.
    begin
      insert into public.orders (branch_id, table_id, order_type, customer_name, customer_phone,
        subtotal, total, payment_method, notes, delivery_address, scheduled_for, source,
        delivery_zone_id, delivery_fee, client_key)
      values (p_branch_id, p_table_id, p_order_type::public.order_type_enum, v_name, v_phone, 0, 0,
        nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_payment_method, '')), '')::public.payment_method,
        v_notes, v_address, p_scheduled_for, v_source, v_zone.id, v_fee, v_key)
      returning id into v_order_id;
    exception when unique_violation then
      get stacked diagnostics v_con = constraint_name;
      if v_con is distinct from 'orders_client_key_uniq' then raise; end if;
      select o.id into v_found from public.orders o where o.client_key = v_key and o.branch_id = p_branch_id;
      if v_found is null then raise exception 'client_key_in_use' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      return private.submit_order_reply(v_found, false, v_device);
    end;
    if p_order_type = 'delivery' then
      insert into public.deliveries (order_id, branch_id) values (v_order_id, p_branch_id) on conflict (order_id) do nothing;
    end if;
  elsif v_notes is not null then
    update public.orders set notes = left(concat_ws(E'\n', nullif(notes, ''), v_notes), 2000) where id = v_order_id;
  end if;

  for v_item in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_clean) loop
    v_qty := (v_item->>'quantity')::int;
    v_price := (v_item->>'unit_price')::numeric;
    v_batch := v_batch + (v_price * v_qty);
    insert into public.order_items (order_id, menu_item_id, name_snapshot, price_snapshot,
      quantity, pax_size, unit_price, total_price, notes, guest_name, device_id, modifiers, client_key)
    values (v_order_id, (v_item->>'menu_item_id')::uuid, v_item->>'name', v_price,
      v_qty, v_item->>'pax_size', v_price, v_price * v_qty, v_item->>'notes', v_name, v_device,
      coalesce(v_item->'modifiers', '[]'::jsonb), v_key);
  end loop;

  if not v_staff then
    perform private.remember_device(v_device, p_branch_id, v_name, v_phone, v_batch, v_clean);
  end if;

  select order_number, track_token into v_number, v_token from public.orders where id = v_order_id;
  select is_flagged into v_flagged from public.customer_devices where device_id = v_device;

  return jsonb_build_object(
    'id', v_order_id, 'order_number', v_number, 'track_token', v_token,
    'joined_existing', v_appended, 'device_flagged', coalesce(v_flagged, false),
    'total',    (select total    from public.orders where id = v_order_id),
    'subtotal', (select subtotal from public.orders where id = v_order_id),
    'delivery_fee', v_fee);
end $$;
revoke all on function public.submit_order(uuid, text, jsonb, uuid, text, text, text, text, text, timestamptz, text, boolean, text, uuid, text) from public;
grant execute on function public.submit_order(uuid, text, jsonb, uuid, text, text, text, text, text, timestamptz, text, boolean, text, uuid, text) to anon, authenticated;

comment on function public.submit_order(uuid, text, jsonb, uuid, text, text, text, text, text, timestamptz, text, boolean, text, uuid, text) is
  'The one door for orders (web, QR, counter). Prices on the server. p_client_key makes a retry return the original order instead of a second one (sql/41).';
````

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
````

<a id="50"></a>

## 50. 43-bir-invoices.sql

`sql/43-bir-invoices.sql` · 191 lines · md5 `d93f9ab307fe6e62307449c19611664e`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 43 · BIR invoices written by hand (the booklet), recorded here
--
-- TPN OS has no BIR Permit to Use, so the official invoice is still
-- written from the BIR-registered booklet. This keeps the record:
--   orders.invoice_no     the booklet number written for that order
--   bir_day_invoices      the end-of-day invoice for small sales
-- and tells staff which sales need one (invoice_day_summary):
--   VAT-registered        every sale
--   not VAT-registered    any sale over the threshold (₱500, RR 7-2024
--                         as amended by RR 11-2024) or when the customer
--                         asks; the rest go on one invoice at day's end
-- site_settings 'bir.vat_registered' = 'yes' | 'no' (blank = not set,
-- treated as not VAT-registered and flagged on screen).
-- Additive only. Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

alter table public.orders add column if not exists invoice_no text;
alter table public.orders add column if not exists invoice_at timestamptz;
alter table public.orders add column if not exists invoice_by uuid references public.staff(id) on delete set null;

-- Only record_order_invoice writes these, so every change is audited.
create or replace function private.trg_guard_invoice_no()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if (new.invoice_no is distinct from old.invoice_no
      or new.invoice_at is distinct from old.invoice_at
      or new.invoice_by is distinct from old.invoice_by)
     and coalesce(current_setting('tpn.invoice', true), 'off') <> 'on' then
    raise exception 'invoice_no_is_set_by_rpc: use record_order_invoice' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_guard_invoice_no() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_guard_invoice_no on public.orders;
create trigger trg_guard_invoice_no before update on public.orders
  for each row execute function private.trg_guard_invoice_no();

create table if not exists public.bir_day_invoices (
  id            uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  branch_id     uuid not null references public.branches(id) on delete cascade,
  business_date date not null,
  invoice_no    text not null check (length(btrim(invoice_no)) between 1 and 40),
  amount        numeric(12,2) not null check (amount > 0),
  recorded_by   uuid references public.staff(id) on delete set null,
  recorded_at   timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (branch_id, business_date, invoice_no)
);
comment on table public.bir_day_invoices is
  'End-of-day BIR invoice(s) covering small sales that got no invoice of their own. Written only through record_day_invoice.';
alter table public.bir_day_invoices enable row level security;
revoke all on public.bir_day_invoices from anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.bir_day_invoices to authenticated;
drop policy if exists bir_day_invoices_read on public.bir_day_invoices;
create policy bir_day_invoices_read on public.bir_day_invoices for select to authenticated
  using (private.can('payments.take') or private.can('finance.view'));

create or replace function private.bir_threshold()
returns numeric language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$ select 500::numeric $$;

create or replace function private.bir_vat_registered()
returns boolean language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select lower(coalesce(s.value #>> '{}', '')) in ('yes', 'true', 'y', 'oo')
    from public.site_settings s where s.key = 'bir.vat_registered'
$$;
revoke all on function private.bir_vat_registered() from public, anon, authenticated;

-- One order's booklet invoice number. Blank clears a mistake. Audited.
create or replace function public.record_order_invoice(p_order_id uuid, p_invoice_no text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  o     public.orders%rowtype;
  v_no  text := nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_invoice_no, '')), '');
begin
  if not private.can('payments.take') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_no is not null and length(v_no) > 40 then
    raise exception 'invoice_no_too_long' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  select * into o from public.orders where id = p_order_id for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'order_not_found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  if o.branch_id is distinct from private.my_branch() and private.my_role() not in ('admin', 'director', 'ceo') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: other branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  perform set_config('tpn.invoice', 'on', true);
  update public.orders
     set invoice_no = v_no,
         invoice_at = case when v_no is null then null else now() end,
         invoice_by = case when v_no is null then null else auth.uid() end
   where id = p_order_id;
  perform set_config('tpn.invoice', 'off', true);

  insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
  values (auth.uid(), private.my_role(), 'invoice.record', 'order', p_order_id,
          jsonb_build_object('order_number', o.order_number, 'invoice_no', v_no, 'was', o.invoice_no));
  return jsonb_build_object('order_id', p_order_id, 'invoice_no', v_no);
end $$;
revoke all on function public.record_order_invoice(uuid, text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.record_order_invoice(uuid, text) to authenticated;

-- The end-of-day invoice for the small sales. Audited.
create or replace function public.record_day_invoice(p_date date, p_invoice_no text, p_amount numeric, p_branch uuid default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_branch uuid := coalesce(p_branch, private.my_branch());
  v_no     text := nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_invoice_no, '')), '');
  v_id     uuid;
begin
  if not (private.can('payments.take') or private.can('finance.view')) then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_branch is distinct from private.my_branch() and private.my_role() not in ('admin', 'director', 'ceo') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: other branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_no is null or length(v_no) > 40 then
    raise exception 'invoice_no_required' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if coalesce(p_amount, 0) <= 0 then
    raise exception 'amount_must_be_positive' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  insert into public.bir_day_invoices (branch_id, business_date, invoice_no, amount, recorded_by)
  values (v_branch, p_date, v_no, round(p_amount, 2), auth.uid())
  returning id into v_id;

  insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
  values (auth.uid(), private.my_role(), 'invoice.day', 'bir_day_invoice', v_id,
          jsonb_build_object('date', p_date, 'invoice_no', v_no, 'amount', round(p_amount, 2)));
  return jsonb_build_object('id', v_id);
end $$;
revoke all on function public.record_day_invoice(date, text, numeric, uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.record_day_invoice(date, text, numeric, uuid) to authenticated;

-- What the day still needs. Paid, not cancelled, placed that Manila day.
create or replace function public.invoice_day_summary(p_date date default null, p_branch uuid default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_branch uuid := coalesce(p_branch, private.my_branch());
  v_date   date := coalesce(p_date, (now() at time zone 'Asia/Manila')::date);
  v_lo     timestamptz := (v_date::timestamp at time zone 'Asia/Manila');
  v_hi     timestamptz := ((v_date + 1)::timestamp at time zone 'Asia/Manila');
  v_vat    boolean := private.bir_vat_registered();
  v_set    boolean := exists (select 1 from public.site_settings s
                               where s.key = 'bir.vat_registered' and nullif(s.value #>> '{}', '') is not null);
  v_min    numeric := private.bir_threshold();
  v        jsonb;
begin
  if not (private.can('payments.take') or private.can('finance.view')) then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  with day as (
    select o.id, o.order_number, o.total, o.invoice_no
      from public.orders o
     where o.branch_id = v_branch and o.status <> 'cancelled' and o.payment_status = 'paid'
       and o.placed_at >= v_lo and o.placed_at < v_hi)
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'date', v_date,
    'vat_registered', v_vat,
    'vat_status_set', v_set,
    'threshold', v_min,
    'invoiced', (select count(*) from day where invoice_no is not null),
    'missing', coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', d.id, 'order_number', d.order_number, 'total', d.total)
                                          order by d.order_number)
                           from day d where d.invoice_no is null and (v_vat or d.total > v_min)), '[]'::jsonb),
    'small_total', case when v_vat then 0
                        else coalesce((select sum(d.total) from day d where d.invoice_no is null and d.total <= v_min), 0) end,
    'small_count', case when v_vat then 0
                        else (select count(*) from day d where d.invoice_no is null and d.total <= v_min) end,
    'day_invoices', coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('invoice_no', b.invoice_no, 'amount', b.amount,
                                                                  'recorded_at', b.recorded_at) order by b.recorded_at)
                                from public.bir_day_invoices b
                               where b.branch_id = v_branch and b.business_date = v_date), '[]'::jsonb)
  ) into v;
  return v;
end $$;
revoke all on function public.invoice_day_summary(date, uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.invoice_day_summary(date, uuid) to authenticated;

insert into public.site_settings (key, value) values ('bir.vat_registered', null)
on conflict (key) do nothing;
````

<a id="51"></a>

## 51. 44-staff-rank-guard.sql

`sql/44-staff-rank-guard.sql` · 101 lines · md5 `5ac629adafc9ddcf145ffebdef25fa06`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 44 — nobody climbs the ladder on their own
--
-- staff_admin_update checked who was calling (admin+), never what the
-- row became. An admin could PATCH their own row to role = 'ceo', or
-- demote the CEO and the director, straight through the REST API.
-- create-staff already refused "a role above yours"; this closes the
-- direct-update path, and the same hole in permission overrides
-- (roles.edit holders could grant themselves any key).
--
-- Rules, for a signed-in caller below CEO:
--   * your own role, custom access role and employment status are
--     changed by someone above you, never by you
--   * you can't edit or delete someone at or above your own tier
--   * you can't give anyone a tier above your own
--   * you can't set permission overrides for yourself or for someone
--     at or above your tier
-- The CEO keeps every key (same lockout rule as private.can). Calls
-- with no auth.uid() — the SQL editor, the service-role edge function,
-- sign-up — are not affected.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

create or replace function private.guard_staff_rank()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare me public.staff_role;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null then return coalesce(new, old); end if;
  select role into me from public.staff where id = auth.uid();
  if me = 'ceo' then return coalesce(new, old); end if;
  if me is null then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: not a staff account' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then
    if old.id = auth.uid() or old.role >= me then
      raise exception 'staff_outranks_you: % is at or above your level', old.role using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    return old;
  end if;

  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    if new.role > me then
      raise exception 'role_above_yours: only someone at % or above can give that role', new.role using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    return new;
  end if;

  if new.role is distinct from old.role
     or new.access_role_id is distinct from old.access_role_id
     or new.employment_status is distinct from old.employment_status then
    if old.id = auth.uid() then
      raise exception 'staff_self_change: your own role, access and status are changed by someone above you' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    if old.role >= me then
      raise exception 'staff_outranks_you: % is at or above your level', old.role using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    if new.role > me then
      raise exception 'role_above_yours: only someone at % or above can give that role', new.role using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;

drop trigger if exists trg_guard_staff_rank on public.staff;
create trigger trg_guard_staff_rank
  before insert or update or delete on public.staff
  for each row execute function private.guard_staff_rank();

create or replace function private.guard_override_rank()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare me public.staff_role; target public.staff_role; sid uuid;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null then return coalesce(new, old); end if;
  select role into me from public.staff where id = auth.uid();
  if me = 'ceo' then return coalesce(new, old); end if;
  sid := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then old.staff_id else new.staff_id end;
  if sid = auth.uid() or (tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.staff_id = auth.uid()) then
    raise exception 'override_self: someone above you sets your own permissions' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select role into target from public.staff where id = sid;
  if me is null or target >= me then
    raise exception 'staff_outranks_you: % is at or above your level', target using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return coalesce(new, old);
end $$;

drop trigger if exists trg_guard_override_rank on public.staff_permission_overrides;
create trigger trg_guard_override_rank
  before insert or update or delete on public.staff_permission_overrides
  for each row execute function private.guard_override_rank();

revoke all on function private.guard_staff_rank()    from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke all on function private.guard_override_rank() from public, anon, authenticated;
````

<a id="52"></a>

## 52. 45-printer-permission.sql

`sql/45-printer-permission.sql` · 15 lines · md5 `4948123e48bf2166aa3c8162579a5cbc`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 45 — printer tools are for managers (Oct 22, 2026)
--
-- The counter's printer menu has test prints, the no-sale drawer
-- button, auto-print and the kitchen printer's address. The owner
-- wants those kept from regular staff: they still see whether the
-- printer is ready and can tap Reconnect.
--
-- Nothing on the server uses this key yet (printing and the no-sale
-- button are on the device); it decides what the menu shows, and the
-- owner can hand it to any role in the roles editor.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

select private.add_permission('printer.manage', 'Administration', 'Printer tools', 'Mga gamit ng printer',
  'Test prints, the no-sale drawer button, auto-print and the kitchen printer address on the counter.', 'manager', 95);
````

<a id="53"></a>

## 53. 46-my-notifications-only.sql

`sql/46-my-notifications-only.sql` · 51 lines · md5 `a4f23cb8ca88feb1b2c418c9114afe67`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 46 — the bell shows only my notifications (Oct 22, 2026)
--
-- notif_read lets admin and up read every notification, including
-- someone else's personal ones (a void request sent to the CEO, say).
-- my_notifications / my_unread_counts listed everything readable, but
-- mark_read can only mark my own or the shared branch rows, so an
-- admin's bell kept other people's notifications unread forever: "read
-- notifications come back after a refresh" (owner, Oct 22).
--
-- Now both list my own plus the shared ones. The table policy is
-- unchanged (admin screens may still read all of them).
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

create or replace function public.my_notifications(p_limit int default 100, p_unread_only boolean default false)
returns setof jsonb
language sql stable security invoker set search_path = ''
as $$
  select to_jsonb(n) || jsonb_build_object('read_at',
           coalesce(case when n.user_id is not null then n.read_at end, r.read_at,
                    case when n.user_id is null then n.read_at end),
           'shared', n.user_id is null)
    from public.notifications n
    left join public.read_receipts r on r.kind = 'notification' and r.item_id = n.id and r.user_id = auth.uid()
   where r.dismissed_at is null
     and (n.user_id is null or n.user_id = auth.uid())
     and (not p_unread_only or coalesce(case when n.user_id is not null then n.read_at end, r.read_at,
                                        case when n.user_id is null then n.read_at end) is null)
   order by n.created_at desc
   limit least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 100), 1), 500)
$$;

create or replace function public.my_unread_counts()
returns jsonb
language sql stable security invoker set search_path = ''
as $$
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'notifications', (select count(*) from public.notifications n
                        left join public.read_receipts r on r.kind = 'notification' and r.item_id = n.id and r.user_id = auth.uid()
                       where r.dismissed_at is null
                         and (n.user_id is null or n.user_id = auth.uid())
                         and coalesce(case when n.user_id is not null then n.read_at end, r.read_at,
                                      case when n.user_id is null then n.read_at end) is null),
    'messages', (select count(*) from public.messages m
                   left join public.read_receipts r on r.kind = 'message' and r.item_id = m.id and r.user_id = auth.uid()
                  where (m.to_staff_id = auth.uid() or m.to_staff_id is null)
                    and m.from_staff_id is distinct from auth.uid()
                    and r.dismissed_at is null
                    and coalesce(case when m.to_staff_id is not null then m.read_at end, r.read_at,
                                 case when m.to_staff_id is null then m.read_at end) is null))
$$;
````
