````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 47 — orders have one door: submit_order (Oct 22, 2026)
--
-- Found in the Oct 22 full test: policy orders_auth_create still let any
-- signed-in staff account (dining tier up) insert a row into orders
-- directly through the API — any total, already 'paid' and 'completed',
-- no items, no payment. Checked on production in a rolled-back
-- transaction: a dining account inserted a ₱1 order marked paid.
--
-- Nothing in the app inserts into orders directly: the website, the QR
-- menu, the counter and the offline outbox all call submit_order
-- (SECURITY DEFINER, server-side prices, idempotency key), which runs as
-- the table owner and is not affected by this change.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

drop policy if exists orders_auth_create on public.orders;
drop policy if exists orders_anon_create on public.orders;
revoke insert on public.orders from anon, authenticated;
````

<a id="55"></a>

## 55. 48-counter-money-locks.sql

`sql/48-counter-money-locks.sql` · 379 lines · md5 `d8057de2fd96fc69610756b07498095e`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 48 — counter money locks (Oct 22, 2026)
--
-- Three gaps from the Oct 22 review, each checked on production in a
-- rolled-back transaction before this file was written:
--
-- 1. A PAID BILL COULD STILL CHANGE. A ₱495 bill paid in cash was voided
--    down to ₱0 by void_order_item and still read "paid". remove_discount,
--    unvoid_order_item, approve_void_request and a supervisor's direct
--    UPDATE of discount_amount / service_charge could move it too.
--    Now: once payment_status = 'paid', nothing may change the bill's
--    money. Every path raises order_already_paid (the token apply_discount
--    already used). The fix for a wrong paid bill is a refund (refund_check),
--    which reopens the bill; after that it can be edited again.
--      • trg_guard_order_update: total, subtotal, discount_amount,
--        service_charge, delivery_fee frozen on a paid bill, for every
--        caller (the SQL API too). This alone catches anything that
--        recomputes the total.
--      • trg_order_items_paid_lock: void / un-void / price change of a line
--        on a paid bill (void_order_item, unvoid_order_item,
--        approve_void_request) refused with the clear token, before the
--        recompute runs.
--      • trg_applied_discounts_paid_lock: a discount added or removed on a
--        paid bill (remove_discount) refused.
--      • trg_void_requests_paid_lock: no new void request on a paid bill
--        (it could never be approved; the manager would only see a dead
--        request). Requests filed before the bill was paid stay; approving
--        them is refused, denying works.
--
-- 2. HAND-ENTERED BANK SKIPPED THE SECOND PERSON. record_manual_payment
--    (and settle_table_manual) with method 'bank' wrote a completed
--    payment and marked the order paid. Now a bank row from either is
--    written 'pending', exactly like record_bank_transfer: it counts only
--    after confirm_bank_transfer, which a different person must do
--    (manager+ may confirm their own — unchanged rule). GCash, Maya,
--    QR Ph and card stay "received at once".
--
-- 3. CASH WITHOUT AN OPEN SHIFT. trg_payment_cash_session (sql/32) only
--    filled in an open drawer when there was one; with none, the cash was
--    recorded with no drawer and never appeared in any X/Z read. Checked on
--    production (rolled back): record_cash_payment with no open shift
--    succeeded, cash_session_id null, order paid.
--    Now a cash PAYMENT must belong to a shift of the order's branch:
--      • no shift given → the open one is used; none open → no_open_shift.
--      • a shift given that is still open → used.
--      • a shift given that has since closed → the open one is used instead,
--        EXCEPT an offline replay (note starts "offline OFF-", written by
--        modules/offline.js) of a shift closed in the last 3 days: that
--        cash was in the drawer when it was counted, so it stays with the
--        shift it was taken in and that shift's stored expected cash and
--        variance are corrected (audited as cash_session.late_offline_cash).
--      • a shift of another branch, or unknown → bad_cash_session.
--    This covers record_cash_payment, settle_table_cash and the offline
--    replay (which calls record_cash_payment). Cash REFUNDS are unchanged
--    (still attached to the open shift when there is one).
--
-- New: my_shift() — the open shift of the caller's branch, for any account
-- that takes payments, so the counter can show "Open shift" instead of the
-- cash keypad (my_open_cash_session needs cash.drawer).
--
-- Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- ── 1. Paid bills are frozen ─────────────────────────────────
create or replace function public.trg_guard_order_update()
returns trigger
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  r public.staff_role;
  settling boolean := coalesce(current_setting('tpn.settle', true), 'off') = 'on';
begin
  r := private.my_role();

  -- sql/48: a paid bill's money does not move, for anyone. Refund first.
  if old.payment_status = 'paid' and not settling
     and (new.total is distinct from old.total
          or new.subtotal is distinct from old.subtotal
          or coalesce(new.discount_amount, 0) is distinct from coalesce(old.discount_amount, 0)
          or coalesce(new.service_charge, 0) is distinct from coalesce(old.service_charge, 0)
          or coalesce(new.delivery_fee, 0) is distinct from coalesce(old.delivery_fee, 0)) then
    raise exception 'order_already_paid: refund first, then change the bill' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  if (new.total    is distinct from old.total
      or new.subtotal is distinct from old.subtotal)
     and coalesce(current_setting('tpn.recompute', true), 'off') <> 'on' then
    raise exception
      'totals_are_derived: change the order items or the discount instead'
      using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  if r is null then
    return new;
  end if;

  if new.status = 'cancelled'
     and old.status is distinct from 'cancelled'
     and not private.has_role('manager') then
    raise exception 'cancel_requires_manager' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  if not settling and not private.has_role('supervisor')
     and (coalesce(new.discount_amount, 0) is distinct from coalesce(old.discount_amount, 0)
          or coalesce(new.service_charge, 0) is distinct from coalesce(old.service_charge, 0)) then
    raise exception 'adjustments_require_supervisor' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  -- Payment state is derived from public.payments. Nobody types it in,
  -- not even a manager: record a payment instead.
  if not settling
     and (new.payment_status is distinct from old.payment_status
          or new.payment_reference is distinct from old.payment_reference
          or new.payment_method is distinct from old.payment_method and old.payment_status = 'paid') then
    raise exception 'payment_state_is_derived: record a payment instead' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  if r in ('kitchen_display', 'dine_in_display') then
    if (to_jsonb(new) - '{status,confirmed_at,ready_at,served_at,updated_at}'::text[])
       is distinct from
       (to_jsonb(old) - '{status,confirmed_at,ready_at,served_at,updated_at}'::text[])
    then
      raise exception 'display_may_only_advance_status' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    if new.status in ('completed', 'cancelled')
       and old.status is distinct from new.status then
      raise exception 'display_cannot_close_orders' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
  end if;

  return new;
end $$;
revoke execute on function public.trg_guard_order_update() from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.order_paid(p_order_id uuid)
returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select coalesce((select payment_status = 'paid' from public.orders where id = p_order_id), false)
$$;
revoke all on function private.order_paid(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- Lines: void, un-void or a price change on a paid bill.
create or replace function private.trg_order_items_paid_lock()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if (new.voided_at is distinct from old.voided_at
      or new.quantity is distinct from old.quantity
      or new.unit_price is distinct from old.unit_price
      or new.total_price is distinct from old.total_price)
     and private.order_paid(old.order_id) then
    raise exception 'order_already_paid: refund first, then void' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_order_items_paid_lock() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_order_items_paid_lock on public.order_items;
create trigger trg_order_items_paid_lock before update on public.order_items
  for each row execute function private.trg_order_items_paid_lock();

-- Discounts: added or removed on a paid bill.
create or replace function private.trg_applied_discounts_paid_lock()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new is not distinct from old then return new; end if;
  if private.order_paid(case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then old.order_id else new.order_id end) then
    raise exception 'order_already_paid: refund first, then change the discount' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return coalesce(new, old);
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_applied_discounts_paid_lock() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_applied_discounts_paid_lock on public.applied_discounts;
create trigger trg_applied_discounts_paid_lock before insert or update or delete on public.applied_discounts
  for each row execute function private.trg_applied_discounts_paid_lock();

-- Void requests: none filed against a paid bill.
create or replace function private.trg_void_requests_paid_lock()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if private.order_paid(new.order_id) then
    raise exception 'order_already_paid: this bill is paid; refund instead' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_void_requests_paid_lock() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_void_requests_paid_lock on public.void_requests;
create trigger trg_void_requests_paid_lock before insert on public.void_requests
  for each row execute function private.trg_void_requests_paid_lock();

-- ── 2. Hand-entered bank waits for a second person ───────────
create or replace function public.record_manual_payment(
  p_order_id uuid, p_method text, p_reference text,
  p_amount numeric default null, p_tip numeric default 0, p_note text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  o       public.orders%rowtype;
  v_m     text := private.manual_method(p_method);
  v_ref   text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_reference, '')), ''), 120);
  v_due   numeric(10,2);
  v_amt   numeric(10,2);
  v_tip   numeric(10,2) := round(coalesce(p_tip, 0), 2);
  v_bank  boolean;
  v_id    uuid;
  v_state text;
begin
  if not private.can('payments.take') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_m is null then raise exception 'bad_method'; end if;
  if v_ref is null or length(v_ref) < 3 then raise exception 'reference_required'; end if;
  select * into o from public.orders where id = p_order_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'order_not_found'; end if;
  perform private.assert_order_access(o);
  if o.status = 'cancelled' then raise exception 'order_cancelled'; end if;
  v_due := private.amount_due(o.id);
  if v_due <= 0 then raise exception 'nothing_due'; end if;
  v_amt := round(coalesce(p_amount, v_due), 2);
  if v_amt <= 0 or v_amt > v_due then raise exception 'bad_amount: due is %', v_due; end if;
  if v_tip < 0 or v_tip > 100000 then raise exception 'bad_tip'; end if;

  -- sql/48: a bank transfer is money only once someone else has seen it
  -- in the bank app (confirm_bank_transfer), same as record_bank_transfer.
  v_bank := v_m = 'bank';
  insert into public.payments (order_id, branch_id, method, status, amount, tip, reference, note,
                               created_by, confirmed_by, confirmed_at)
  values (o.id, o.branch_id, v_m, case when v_bank then 'pending' else 'completed' end, v_amt, v_tip, v_ref,
          left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_note, '')), ''), 500), auth.uid(),
          case when v_bank then null else auth.uid() end, case when v_bank then null else now() end)
  returning id into v_id;

  v_state := private.settle_order(o.id);
  return jsonb_build_object('payment_id', v_id, 'payment_status', v_state, 'amount_due', private.amount_due(o.id),
                            'pending', v_bank);
end $$;

create or replace function public.settle_table_manual(
  p_order_ids uuid[], p_method text, p_reference text, p_tip numeric default 0, p_note text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  o       public.orders%rowtype;
  v_m     text := private.manual_method(p_method);
  v_ref   text := left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_reference, '')), ''), 120);
  v_tip   numeric(10,2) := round(coalesce(p_tip, 0), 2);
  v_total numeric(10,2) := 0;
  v_due   numeric(10,2);
  v_first boolean := true;
  v_paid  int := 0;
  v_bank  boolean;
begin
  if not private.can('payments.take') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_m is null then raise exception 'bad_method'; end if;
  if v_ref is null or length(v_ref) < 3 then raise exception 'reference_required'; end if;
  if p_order_ids is null or cardinality(p_order_ids) = 0 then raise exception 'nothing_due'; end if;
  if cardinality(p_order_ids) > 50 then raise exception 'too_many_orders'; end if;
  if v_tip < 0 or v_tip > 100000 then raise exception 'bad_tip'; end if;
  v_bank := v_m = 'bank';

  for o in select * from public.orders where id = any(p_order_ids) order by id for update loop
    perform private.assert_order_access(o);
    if o.status <> 'cancelled' then v_total := v_total + private.amount_due(o.id); end if;
  end loop;
  if v_total <= 0 then raise exception 'nothing_due'; end if;

  for o in select * from public.orders where id = any(p_order_ids) and status <> 'cancelled' order by created_at loop
    v_due := private.amount_due(o.id);
    continue when v_due <= 0;
    insert into public.payments (order_id, branch_id, method, status, amount, tip, reference, note,
                                 created_by, confirmed_by, confirmed_at)
    values (o.id, o.branch_id, v_m, case when v_bank then 'pending' else 'completed' end, v_due,
            case when v_first then v_tip else 0 end, v_ref,
            coalesce(left(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_note, '')), ''), 500), 'Table settled together'),
            auth.uid(), case when v_bank then null else auth.uid() end, case when v_bank then null else now() end);
    perform private.settle_order(o.id);
    v_first := false;
    v_paid := v_paid + 1;
  end loop;
  return jsonb_build_object('orders_paid', case when v_bank then 0 else v_paid end, 'orders_pending', case when v_bank then v_paid else 0 end,
                            'amount', v_total, 'tip', v_tip, 'method', v_m, 'pending', v_bank);
end $$;

revoke all on function public.record_manual_payment(uuid, text, text, numeric, numeric, text) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.settle_table_manual(uuid[], text, text, numeric, text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.record_manual_payment(uuid, text, text, numeric, numeric, text) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.settle_table_manual(uuid[], text, text, numeric, text) to authenticated;

-- ── 3. No cash without an open shift ─────────────────────────
create or replace function private.trg_payment_cash_session()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare s public.cash_sessions%rowtype;
begin
  if new.method <> 'cash' then return new; end if;

  if new.kind = 'refund' then
    -- Unchanged: a cash refund goes against the open drawer when there is one.
    if new.cash_session_id is null then
      select id into new.cash_session_id from public.cash_sessions
       where branch_id = new.branch_id and status = 'open'
       order by opened_at desc limit 1;
    end if;
    return new;
  end if;

  if new.cash_session_id is not null then
    select * into s from public.cash_sessions where id = new.cash_session_id;
    if not found or s.branch_id is distinct from new.branch_id then
      raise exception 'bad_cash_session' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    if s.status = 'open' then return new; end if;
    -- Cash taken offline while this shift was open, replayed after it was
    -- closed: it was in the drawer when the drawer was counted, so it stays
    -- with that shift (the AFTER trigger corrects the shift's figures).
    if coalesce(new.note, '') like 'offline OFF-%' and s.closed_at > now() - interval '3 days' then
      return new;
    end if;
    new.cash_session_id := null;   -- a stale shift on a screen: use the open one
  end if;

  select id into new.cash_session_id from public.cash_sessions
   where branch_id = new.branch_id and status = 'open'
   order by (register_label = 'Counter') desc, opened_at desc limit 1;
  if new.cash_session_id is null then
    raise exception 'no_open_shift: open a shift before taking cash' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_payment_cash_session() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_payment_cash_session on public.payments;
create trigger trg_payment_cash_session before insert on public.payments
  for each row execute function private.trg_payment_cash_session();

-- Late offline cash on a closed shift: the stored Z read is put right.
create or replace function private.trg_payment_late_offline_cash()
returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare s public.cash_sessions%rowtype; v_add numeric(10,2);
begin
  if new.method <> 'cash' or new.kind <> 'payment' or new.status <> 'completed' or new.cash_session_id is null then
    return null;
  end if;
  select * into s from public.cash_sessions where id = new.cash_session_id for update;
  if not found or s.status <> 'closed' then return null; end if;
  v_add := new.amount + new.tip;
  update public.cash_sessions
     set expected_cash = coalesce(expected_cash, 0) + v_add,
         variance = counted_cash - (coalesce(expected_cash, 0) + v_add)
   where id = s.id;
  insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, before_state, after_state, metadata)
  values (auth.uid(), private.my_role(), 'cash_session.late_offline_cash', 'cash_session', s.id,
          jsonb_build_object('expected_cash', s.expected_cash, 'variance', s.variance),
          jsonb_build_object('expected_cash', coalesce(s.expected_cash, 0) + v_add, 'variance', s.counted_cash - (coalesce(s.expected_cash, 0) + v_add)),
          jsonb_build_object('payment_id', new.id, 'order_id', new.order_id, 'amount', v_add, 'note', new.note));
  return null;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_payment_late_offline_cash() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_payment_late_offline_cash on public.payments;
create trigger trg_payment_late_offline_cash after insert on public.payments
  for each row execute function private.trg_payment_late_offline_cash();

-- The open shift of my branch (the Counter register first), for anyone who
-- takes payments: the counter shows "Open shift" instead of the keypad.
create or replace function public.my_shift()
returns uuid
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if not (private.can('payments.take') or private.can('cash.drawer')) then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: payments.take' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return (select id from public.cash_sessions
           where branch_id = private.my_branch() and status = 'open'
           order by (register_label = 'Counter') desc, opened_at desc limit 1);
end $$;
revoke all on function public.my_shift() from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.my_shift() to authenticated;
````

<a id="56"></a>

## 56. 49-manager-pin.sql

`sql/49-manager-pin.sql` · 467 lines · md5 `70592f5583cdde2bea621d786694e7a4`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 49 — manager PIN at the counter (Oct 22, 2026)
--
-- RECOVERED Nov 7, 2026. Production ran this on Oct 22 (Supabase
-- migrations 49_manager_pin and 49_manager_pin_no_pin_set) but the file
-- never reached GitHub. Both parts below are copied byte for byte from
-- supabase_migrations.schema_migrations on project xjlqfpnzobfqxetgkkai
-- (md5 3f36e509… and ab2e9053…), in the order production ran them.
--
-- A manager (or anyone with voids.approve / payments.refund /
-- printer.manage) sets a 4–6 digit PIN. At the counter a cashier types
-- that PIN to void, discount, refund or pass any permission check
-- without the manager logging in. The action runs as the manager; the
-- audit row keeps the cashier as actor and adds approved_by.
--   private.staff_pins, private.pin_fails (5 misses in 10 min = locked)
--   my_pin_status, set_my_pin, clear_my_pin
--   pin_void_order_item, pin_approve_void_request, pin_apply_discount,
--   pin_remove_discount, pin_refund, pin_check
-- Part 2 makes pin_verify answer no_pin_set when nobody in the branch
-- has a PIN yet. Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- ── Part 1: 49_manager_pin ──────────────────────────────────────
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 49 — manager PIN at the counter (Oct 22, 2026)
-- See sql/49-manager-pin.sql in the repo for the full header.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

create extension if not exists pgcrypto with schema extensions;

create table if not exists private.staff_pins (
  staff_id   uuid primary key references public.staff(id) on delete cascade,
  pin_hash   text not null,
  set_at     timestamptz not null default now()
);
create table if not exists private.pin_fails (
  staff_id uuid not null,
  at       timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index if not exists idx_pin_fails on private.pin_fails (staff_id, at desc);
revoke all on private.staff_pins, private.pin_fails from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.can_staff(p_staff uuid, p_key text)
returns boolean
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_role_id uuid;
  v_tier    public.staff_role;
  v_allowed boolean;
begin
  select role, access_role_id into v_tier, v_role_id
    from public.staff
   where id = p_staff and coalesce(employment_status, 'active') = 'active';
  if v_tier is null then return false; end if;
  if v_tier = 'ceo' then return true; end if;

  select allowed into v_allowed
    from public.staff_permission_overrides
   where staff_id = p_staff and permission_key = p_key;
  if v_allowed is not null then return v_allowed; end if;

  if v_role_id is not null then
    select arp.allowed into v_allowed
      from public.access_role_permissions arp
      join public.access_roles r on r.id = arp.role_id
     where arp.role_id = v_role_id and arp.permission_key = p_key and r.is_active;
    return coalesce(v_allowed, false);
  end if;

  select arp.allowed into v_allowed
    from public.access_role_permissions arp
    join public.access_roles r on r.id = arp.role_id
   where r.key = v_tier::text and r.is_system and arp.permission_key = p_key;
  return coalesce(v_allowed, false);
end $$;
revoke all on function private.can_staff(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.pin_eligible(p_staff uuid)
returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select exists (
    select 1 from public.staff s
     where s.id = p_staff and coalesce(s.employment_status, 'active') = 'active'
       and (array_position(enum_range(null::public.staff_role), s.role)
              >= array_position(enum_range(null::public.staff_role), 'manager'::public.staff_role)
            or private.can_staff(s.id, 'voids.approve')
            or private.can_staff(s.id, 'payments.refund')
            or private.can_staff(s.id, 'printer.manage')))
$$;
revoke all on function private.pin_eligible(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.pin_recent_fails(p_staff uuid)
returns int
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select count(*)::int from private.pin_fails
   where staff_id = p_staff and at > now() - interval '10 minutes'
$$;
revoke all on function private.pin_recent_fails(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function public.my_pin_status()
returns jsonb
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'eligible', coalesce(private.pin_eligible(auth.uid()), false),
    'has_pin',  exists (select 1 from private.staff_pins where staff_id = auth.uid()),
    'set_at',   (select set_at from private.staff_pins where staff_id = auth.uid()))
$$;
revoke all on function public.my_pin_status() from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.my_pin_status() to authenticated;

create or replace function public.set_my_pin(p_pin text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  me    public.staff%rowtype;
  v_pin text := btrim(coalesce(p_pin, ''));
  had   boolean;
begin
  select * into me from public.staff
   where id = auth.uid() and coalesce(employment_status, 'active') = 'active';
  if not found then raise exception 'no_staff_profile' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if not private.pin_eligible(me.id) then
    raise exception 'pin_not_allowed' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if private.pin_recent_fails(me.id) >= 5 then
    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'pin_locked');
  end if;
  if v_pin !~ '^[0-9]{4,6}$' then raise exception 'pin_format' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if v_pin ~ '^(.)\1+$' or strpos('01234567890', v_pin) > 0 or strpos('09876543210', v_pin) > 0 then
    raise exception 'pin_too_simple' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  if exists (
    select 1 from private.staff_pins p
      join public.staff s on s.id = p.staff_id
     where s.branch_id is not distinct from me.branch_id
       and p.staff_id <> me.id
       and p.pin_hash = extensions.crypt(v_pin, p.pin_hash)
  ) then
    insert into private.pin_fails (staff_id) values (me.id);
    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'pin_in_use');
  end if;

  had := exists (select 1 from private.staff_pins where staff_id = me.id);
  insert into private.staff_pins (staff_id, pin_hash, set_at)
  values (me.id, extensions.crypt(v_pin, extensions.gen_salt('bf', 8)), now())
  on conflict (staff_id) do update set pin_hash = excluded.pin_hash, set_at = excluded.set_at;

  insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
  values (me.id, me.role, 'pin.set', 'staff', me.id,
          jsonb_build_object('changed', had, 'branch_id', me.branch_id));
  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'changed', had);
end $$;
revoke all on function public.set_my_pin(text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.set_my_pin(text) to authenticated;

create or replace function public.clear_my_pin()
returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare me public.staff%rowtype;
begin
  select * into me from public.staff where id = auth.uid();
  if not found then raise exception 'no_staff_profile' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  delete from private.staff_pins where staff_id = me.id;
  if found then
    insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
    values (me.id, me.role, 'pin.clear', 'staff', me.id, jsonb_build_object('branch_id', me.branch_id));
  end if;
  return true;
end $$;
revoke all on function public.clear_my_pin() from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.clear_my_pin() to authenticated;

create or replace function private.pin_verify(p_pin text, p_key text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  me    public.staff%rowtype;
  mgr   public.staff%rowtype;
  v_pin text := btrim(coalesce(p_pin, ''));
begin
  select * into me from public.staff
   where id = auth.uid() and coalesce(employment_status, 'active') = 'active';
  if not found then raise exception 'no_staff_profile' using errcode = '42501'; end if;

  if private.pin_recent_fails(me.id) >= 5 then
    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'pin_locked');
  end if;

  if v_pin ~ '^[0-9]{4,6}$' then
    select s.* into mgr
      from private.staff_pins p
      join public.staff s on s.id = p.staff_id
     where s.branch_id = me.branch_id
       and coalesce(s.employment_status, 'active') = 'active'
       and p.pin_hash = extensions.crypt(v_pin, p.pin_hash)
     order by p.set_at
     limit 1;
  end if;

  if mgr.id is null then
    insert into private.pin_fails (staff_id) values (me.id);
    insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
    values (me.id, me.role, 'pin.bad', 'staff', me.id,
            jsonb_build_object('permission', p_key, 'misses', private.pin_recent_fails(me.id)));
    if private.pin_recent_fails(me.id) >= 5 then
      return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'pin_locked');
    end if;
    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'bad_pin');
  end if;

  if not private.pin_eligible(mgr.id) or not private.can_staff(mgr.id, p_key) then
    raise exception 'pin_not_allowed: %', p_key using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  delete from private.pin_fails where staff_id = me.id;
  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'caller', me.id, 'staff_id', mgr.id,
                            'approved_by', mgr.id,
                            'approved_by_name', split_part(btrim(mgr.full_name), ' ', 1));
end $$;
revoke all on function private.pin_verify(text, text) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.pin_as_begin(p_v jsonb)
returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_claims jsonb := coalesce(nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')::jsonb, '{}'::jsonb);
begin
  perform set_config('tpn.pin_saved_sub', coalesce(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.sub', true), ''), true);
  perform set_config('tpn.pin_saved_claims', coalesce(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), ''), true);
  perform set_config('tpn.pin_caller', p_v->>'caller', true);
  perform set_config('tpn.pin_approver', p_v->>'staff_id', true);
  perform set_config('tpn.pin_approver_name', coalesce(p_v->>'approved_by_name', ''), true);
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', p_v->>'staff_id', true);
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claims', (v_claims || jsonb_build_object('sub', p_v->>'staff_id'))::text, true);
end $$;
revoke all on function private.pin_as_begin(jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.pin_as_end()
returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', coalesce(current_setting('tpn.pin_saved_sub', true), ''), true);
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claims', coalesce(current_setting('tpn.pin_saved_claims', true), ''), true);
  perform set_config('tpn.pin_caller', '', true);
  perform set_config('tpn.pin_approver', '', true);
  perform set_config('tpn.pin_approver_name', '', true);
end $$;
revoke all on function private.pin_as_end() from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function private.trg_audit_pin_actor()
returns trigger
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare v_caller uuid; v_appr uuid;
begin
  v_appr := nullif(current_setting('tpn.pin_approver', true), '')::uuid;
  if v_appr is null then return new; end if;
  v_caller := nullif(current_setting('tpn.pin_caller', true), '')::uuid;
  if v_caller is null then return new; end if;
  new.actor_id   := v_caller;
  new.actor_role := (select role from public.staff where id = v_caller);
  new.metadata   := coalesce(new.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) || jsonb_build_object(
    'approved_by', v_appr,
    'approved_by_name', nullif(current_setting('tpn.pin_approver_name', true), ''),
    'via', 'manager_pin');
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_audit_pin_actor() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_audit_pin_actor on public.audit_log;
create trigger trg_audit_pin_actor before insert on public.audit_log
  for each row execute function private.trg_audit_pin_actor();

create or replace function private.pin_assert_branch(p_order uuid)
returns void
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from public.orders o join public.staff s on s.id = auth.uid()
                  where o.id = p_order and o.branch_id = s.branch_id) then
    if not exists (select 1 from public.orders where id = p_order) then
      raise exception 'order_not_found' using errcode = 'P0002';
    end if;
    raise exception 'wrong_branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.pin_assert_branch(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;

create or replace function public.pin_void_order_item(p_item uuid, p_reason text, p_pin text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare v jsonb; r public.order_items%rowtype;
begin
  perform private.pin_assert_branch((select order_id from public.order_items where id = p_item));
  v := private.pin_verify(p_pin, 'voids.approve');
  if not (v->>'ok')::boolean then return v; end if;
  perform private.pin_as_begin(v);
  r := public.void_order_item(p_item, p_reason);
  perform private.pin_as_end();
  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'approved_by', v->'approved_by',
                            'approved_by_name', v->'approved_by_name', 'item', to_jsonb(r));
end $$;

create or replace function public.pin_check(p_pin text, p_permission text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare v jsonb;
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from public.permissions where key = p_permission) then
    raise exception 'unknown_permission' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v := private.pin_verify(p_pin, p_permission);
  if not (v->>'ok')::boolean then return v; end if;
  perform private.pin_as_begin(v);
  insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
  values (auth.uid(), private.my_role(), 'pin.approve', 'staff', (v->>'caller')::uuid,
          jsonb_build_object('permission', p_permission));
  perform private.pin_as_end();
  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'approved_by', v->'approved_by',
                            'approved_by_name', v->'approved_by_name');
end $$;
revoke all on function public.pin_check(text, text) from public, anon;

-- ── Part 2: 49_manager_pin_no_pin_set ───────────────────────────
-- sql/49-manager-pin.sql, final pin_verify: no_pin_set when nobody in the branch has a PIN yet.
create or replace function private.pin_verify(p_pin text, p_key text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  me    public.staff%rowtype;
  mgr   public.staff%rowtype;
  v_pin text := btrim(coalesce(p_pin, ''));
begin
  select * into me from public.staff
   where id = auth.uid() and coalesce(employment_status, 'active') = 'active';
  if not found then raise exception 'no_staff_profile' using errcode = '42501'; end if;

  if private.pin_recent_fails(me.id) >= 5 then
    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'pin_locked');
  end if;

  -- Nobody in this branch has a PIN yet: say so (not a miss).
  if not exists (select 1 from private.staff_pins p join public.staff s on s.id = p.staff_id
                  where s.branch_id = me.branch_id and coalesce(s.employment_status, 'active') = 'active') then
    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'no_pin_set');
  end if;

  if v_pin ~ '^[0-9]{4,6}$' then
    select s.* into mgr
      from private.staff_pins p
      join public.staff s on s.id = p.staff_id
     where s.branch_id = me.branch_id
       and coalesce(s.employment_status, 'active') = 'active'
       and p.pin_hash = extensions.crypt(v_pin, p.pin_hash)
     order by p.set_at
     limit 1;
  end if;

  if mgr.id is null then
    insert into private.pin_fails (staff_id) values (me.id);
    insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)
    values (me.id, me.role, 'pin.bad', 'staff', me.id,
            jsonb_build_object('permission', p_key, 'misses', private.pin_recent_fails(me.id)));
    if private.pin_recent_fails(me.id) >= 5 then
      return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'pin_locked');
    end if;
    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'bad_pin');
  end if;

  if not private.pin_eligible(mgr.id) or not private.can_staff(mgr.id, p_key) then
    raise exception 'pin_not_allowed: %', p_key using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  delete from private.pin_fails where staff_id = me.id;
  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'caller', me.id, 'staff_id', mgr.id,
                            'approved_by', mgr.id,
                            'approved_by_name', split_part(btrim(mgr.full_name), ' ', 1));
end $$;
revoke all on function private.pin_verify(text, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
````

## 57. 50-menu-engineering.sql

`sql/50-menu-engineering.sql` · 288 lines · md5 `aa31bea24de806e0f921705ba7478dae`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 50 — menu engineering: food cost per dish, stars and dogs (Oct 22, 2026)
--
-- RECOVERED Nov 7, 2026. Production ran this on Oct 22 (Supabase
-- migrations 50_menu_engineering and 50_menu_engineering_size_names) but
-- the file never reached GitHub. Both parts below are copied byte for
-- byte from supabase_migrations.schema_migrations on project
-- xjlqfpnzobfqxetgkkai (md5 8f15de07… and f5642787…), in order.
--
--   public.menu_item_costs   food cost per dish, or per size (pax_costs)
--   set_menu_item_cost       menu.edit, audited
--   menu_engineering         finance.view; sorts sold dishes into
--                            star / plowhorse / puzzle / dog by
--                            popularity and margin (price when a cost
--                            is missing). Manila days.
-- Part 2 reads old "2-3 pax" order lines as size "2-3". Safe to re-run.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- ── Part 1: 50_menu_engineering ─────────────────────────────────
-- sql/50-menu-engineering.sql (see repo for the full header)
create table if not exists public.menu_item_costs (
  menu_item_id uuid primary key references public.menu_items(id) on delete cascade,
  food_cost    numeric(10,2) check (food_cost is null or (food_cost >= 0 and food_cost < 100000)),
  pax_costs    jsonb check (pax_costs is null or jsonb_typeof(pax_costs) = 'object'),
  updated_at   timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_by   uuid references public.staff(id)
);
alter table public.menu_item_costs enable row level security;
revoke all on public.menu_item_costs from public, anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.menu_item_costs to authenticated;
drop policy if exists menu_item_costs_read on public.menu_item_costs;
create policy menu_item_costs_read on public.menu_item_costs
  for select to authenticated
  using (private.can('menu.edit') or private.can('finance.view'));

create or replace function public.set_menu_item_cost(
  p_item uuid, p_food_cost numeric, p_pax_costs jsonb default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_mi public.menu_items%rowtype;
  v_before jsonb;
  v_after  jsonb;
  v_pax    jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
  k text; v jsonb;
begin
  if not private.can('menu.edit') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: menu.edit' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into v_mi from public.menu_items where id = p_item;
  if not found then raise exception 'unknown_item' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_food_cost is not null and (p_food_cost < 0 or p_food_cost >= 100000) then
    raise exception 'bad_cost: %', p_food_cost using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_pax_costs is not null and jsonb_typeof(p_pax_costs) <> 'null' then
    if jsonb_typeof(p_pax_costs) <> 'object' then
      raise exception 'bad_cost: sizes must be an object' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    for k, v in select * from jsonb_each(p_pax_costs) loop
      if jsonb_typeof(v) = 'null' or (jsonb_typeof(v) = 'string' and btrim(v #>> '{}') = '') then
        continue;
      end if;
      if v_mi.pax_options is null or jsonb_typeof(v_mi.pax_options) <> 'object' or not (v_mi.pax_options ? k) then
        raise exception 'unknown_size: %', k using errcode = '22023';
      end if;
      if (v #>> '{}') !~ '^\s*\d+(\.\d+)?\s*$' or (v #>> '{}')::numeric >= 100000 then
        raise exception 'bad_cost: %', v #>> '{}' using errcode = '22023';
      end if;
      v_pax := v_pax || jsonb_build_object(k, round((v #>> '{}')::numeric, 2));
    end loop;
  end if;

  select to_jsonb(c) into v_before from public.menu_item_costs c where c.menu_item_id = p_item;
  if p_food_cost is null and v_pax = '{}'::jsonb then
    delete from public.menu_item_costs where menu_item_id = p_item;
    v_after := null;
  else
    insert into public.menu_item_costs (menu_item_id, food_cost, pax_costs, updated_at, updated_by)
    values (p_item, round(p_food_cost, 2), nullif(v_pax, '{}'::jsonb), now(), auth.uid())
    on conflict (menu_item_id) do update
      set food_cost = excluded.food_cost, pax_costs = excluded.pax_costs,
          updated_at = excluded.updated_at, updated_by = excluded.updated_by;
    select to_jsonb(c) into v_after from public.menu_item_costs c where c.menu_item_id = p_item;
  end if;

  if v_before is distinct from v_after then
    insert into public.audit_log (actor_id, actor_role, action, entity_type, entity_id, before_state, after_state, metadata)
    values (auth.uid(), private.my_role(), 'menu_item_cost.set', 'menu_items', p_item, v_before, v_after,
            jsonb_build_object('name', v_mi.name));
  end if;
  return coalesce(v_after, jsonb_build_object('menu_item_id', p_item, 'food_cost', null, 'pax_costs', null));
end $$;
revoke all on function public.set_menu_item_cost(uuid, numeric, jsonb) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.set_menu_item_cost(uuid, numeric, jsonb) to authenticated;

create or replace function public.menu_engineering(p_from date, p_to date, p_branch uuid default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_branch uuid := coalesce(p_branch, private.my_branch());
  v_lo timestamptz;
  v_hi timestamptz;
  v jsonb;
begin
  if not private.can('finance.view') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: finance.view' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_branch is not null and p_branch is distinct from private.my_branch()
     and not private.can('settings.manage') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: other branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_from is null or p_to is null or p_from > p_to or p_to - p_from > 366 then
    raise exception 'bad_range' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v_lo := (p_from::timestamp at time zone 'Asia/Manila');
  v_hi := ((p_to + 1)::timestamp at time zone 'Asia/Manila');

  with lines as (
    select oi.menu_item_id, oi.pax_size, oi.quantity, oi.total_price, oi.name_snapshot,
           case when oi.pax_size is null then c.food_cost
                else (c.pax_costs ->> oi.pax_size)::numeric end as unit_cost
      from public.order_items oi
      join public.orders o on o.id = oi.order_id
      left join public.menu_item_costs c on c.menu_item_id = oi.menu_item_id
     where o.branch_id = v_branch
       and o.status = 'completed'
       and o.placed_at >= v_lo and o.placed_at < v_hi
       and oi.voided_at is null
       and oi.menu_item_id is not null
       and oi.quantity > 0
  ), rows as (
    select l.menu_item_id, l.pax_size as size,
           coalesce(max(mi.name), max(l.name_snapshot)) as name, max(mc.name) as category,
           sum(l.quantity)::numeric as qty, sum(l.total_price) as revenue,
           sum(l.quantity * coalesce(l.unit_cost, 0)) as cost,
           bool_or(l.unit_cost is null) as cost_missing
      from lines l
      left join public.menu_items mi on mi.id = l.menu_item_id
      left join public.menu_categories mc on mc.id = mi.category_id
     group by l.menu_item_id, l.pax_size
  ), tot as (
    select count(*) as n, coalesce(sum(qty), 0) as qty, coalesce(sum(revenue), 0) as revenue,
           coalesce(sum(revenue - cost), 0) as margin,
           count(*) filter (where cost_missing) as missing
      from rows
  ), k as (
    select t.*, case when t.missing = 0 then 'margin' else 'price' end as basis,
           case when t.n > 0 then 0.7 / t.n end as pop,
           case when t.missing = 0 then t.margin else t.revenue end / nullif(t.qty, 0) as prof
      from tot t
  ), scored as (
    select r.*, r.qty / nullif(k.qty, 0) as share,
           case when k.basis = 'margin' then (r.revenue - r.cost) / r.qty else r.revenue / r.qty end as val,
           k.pop, k.prof
      from rows r cross join k
  )
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'from', p_from, 'to', p_to, 'branch_id', v_branch,
    'basis', k.basis,
    'dishes', k.n, 'items_sold', k.qty, 'revenue', round(k.revenue, 2),
    'total_margin', case when k.basis = 'margin' then round(k.margin, 2) end,
    'popularity_threshold', round(k.pop, 4),
    'profit_threshold', round(k.prof, 2),
    'cost_missing_count', k.missing,
    'items', coalesce((
      select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'menu_item_id', s.menu_item_id, 'size', s.size, 'name', s.name, 'category', s.category,
               'qty', s.qty, 'revenue', round(s.revenue, 2),
               'share', round(s.share, 4), 'avg_price', round(s.revenue / s.qty, 2),
               'unit_cost', case when not s.cost_missing then round(s.cost / s.qty, 2) end,
               'unit_margin', case when not s.cost_missing then round((s.revenue - s.cost) / s.qty, 2) end,
               'total_margin', case when not s.cost_missing then round(s.revenue - s.cost, 2) end,
               'value', round(s.val, 2),
               'cost_missing', s.cost_missing,
               'popular', s.share >= s.pop, 'profitable', s.val >= s.prof,
               'group', case when s.share >= s.pop and s.val >= s.prof then 'star'
                             when s.share >= s.pop then 'plowhorse'
                             when s.val >= s.prof then 'puzzle'
                             else 'dog' end)
             order by s.qty desc, s.revenue desc, s.name)
        from scored s), '[]'::jsonb))
    into v
    from k;
  return v;
end $$;
revoke all on function public.menu_engineering(date, date, uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.menu_engineering(date, date, uuid) to authenticated;

-- ── Part 2: 50_menu_engineering_size_names ──────────────────────
-- sql/50 (final): menu_engineering reads old "2-3 pax" lines as "2-3".
create or replace function public.menu_engineering(p_from date, p_to date, p_branch uuid default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_branch uuid := coalesce(p_branch, private.my_branch());
  v_lo timestamptz;
  v_hi timestamptz;
  v jsonb;
begin
  if not private.can('finance.view') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: finance.view' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_branch is not null and p_branch is distinct from private.my_branch()
     and not private.can('settings.manage') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: other branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_from is null or p_to is null or p_from > p_to or p_to - p_from > 366 then
    raise exception 'bad_range' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v_lo := (p_from::timestamp at time zone 'Asia/Manila');
  v_hi := ((p_to + 1)::timestamp at time zone 'Asia/Manila');

  with raw as (
    select oi.*, nullif(regexp_replace(btrim(coalesce(oi.pax_size, '')), '\s*pax$', '', 'i'), '') as size_key
      from public.order_items oi
  ), lines as (
    select oi.menu_item_id, oi.size_key as pax_size, oi.quantity, oi.total_price, oi.name_snapshot,
           case when oi.size_key is null then c.food_cost
                else (c.pax_costs ->> oi.size_key)::numeric end as unit_cost
      from raw oi
      join public.orders o on o.id = oi.order_id
      left join public.menu_item_costs c on c.menu_item_id = oi.menu_item_id
     where o.branch_id = v_branch
       and o.status = 'completed'
       and o.placed_at >= v_lo and o.placed_at < v_hi
       and oi.voided_at is null
       and oi.menu_item_id is not null
       and oi.quantity > 0
  ), rows as (
    select l.menu_item_id, l.pax_size as size,
           coalesce(max(mi.name), max(l.name_snapshot)) as name, max(mc.name) as category,
           sum(l.quantity)::numeric as qty, sum(l.total_price) as revenue,
           sum(l.quantity * coalesce(l.unit_cost, 0)) as cost,
           bool_or(l.unit_cost is null) as cost_missing
      from lines l
      left join public.menu_items mi on mi.id = l.menu_item_id
      left join public.menu_categories mc on mc.id = mi.category_id
     group by l.menu_item_id, l.pax_size
  ), tot as (
    select count(*) as n, coalesce(sum(qty), 0) as qty, coalesce(sum(revenue), 0) as revenue,
           coalesce(sum(revenue - cost), 0) as margin,
           count(*) filter (where cost_missing) as missing
      from rows
  ), k as (
    select t.*, case when t.missing = 0 then 'margin' else 'price' end as basis,
           case when t.n > 0 then 0.7 / t.n end as pop,
           case when t.missing = 0 then t.margin else t.revenue end / nullif(t.qty, 0) as prof
      from tot t
  ), scored as (
    select r.*, r.qty / nullif(k.qty, 0) as share,
           case when k.basis = 'margin' then (r.revenue - r.cost) / r.qty else r.revenue / r.qty end as val,
           k.pop, k.prof
      from rows r cross join k
  )
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'from', p_from, 'to', p_to, 'branch_id', v_branch,
    'basis', k.basis,
    'dishes', k.n, 'items_sold', k.qty, 'revenue', round(k.revenue, 2),
    'total_margin', case when k.basis = 'margin' then round(k.margin, 2) end,
    'popularity_threshold', round(k.pop, 4),
    'profit_threshold', round(k.prof, 2),
    'cost_missing_count', k.missing,
    'items', coalesce((
      select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'menu_item_id', s.menu_item_id, 'size', s.size, 'name', s.name, 'category', s.category,
               'qty', s.qty, 'revenue', round(s.revenue, 2),
               'share', round(s.share, 4), 'avg_price', round(s.revenue / s.qty, 2),
               'unit_cost', case when not s.cost_missing then round(s.cost / s.qty, 2) end,
               'unit_margin', case when not s.cost_missing then round((s.revenue - s.cost) / s.qty, 2) end,
               'total_margin', case when not s.cost_missing then round(s.revenue - s.cost, 2) end,
               'value', round(s.val, 2),
               'cost_missing', s.cost_missing,
               'popular', s.share >= s.pop, 'profitable', s.val >= s.prof,
               'group', case when s.share >= s.pop and s.val >= s.prof then 'star'
                             when s.share >= s.pop then 'plowhorse'
                             when s.val >= s.prof then 'puzzle'
                             else 'dog' end)
             order by s.qty desc, s.revenue desc, s.name)
        from scored s), '[]'::jsonb))
    into v
    from k;
  return v;
end $$;
revoke all on function public.menu_engineering(date, date, uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.menu_engineering(date, date, uuid) to authenticated;
````

<a id="58"></a>

## 58. 51-manila-days.sql

`sql/51-manila-days.sql` · 43 lines · md5 `e6aee2fa528fcc09a93339aedc20c524`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 51 — every report's day is the Manila day (Oct 25, 2026)
--
-- (49 manager PIN and 50 menu engineering are on production from the
-- Mac checkout; their files had not reached main when this was written.)
--
-- 1. REPORTS CUT DAYS AT 8 AM MANILA. The database runs on UTC (checked on
--    production: TimeZone = UTC). Wherever a function compares a timestamp
--    with a plain date, or casts a timestamp to a date, Postgres uses that
--    zone, so a "day" ran from 8:00 AM to 8:00 AM Manila time:
--      • finance_summary: sales, COGS, labour (attendance) and expenses
--      • inventory_variance: stock movements
--      • build_payroll_run: days worked (clock_in_at::date)
--      • set_order_number: the date inside TPN-XXX-YYYYMMDD-NNNN, so an
--        order at 7 AM carried yesterday's date
--      • daily_close_report / record_daily_close / record_day_invoice /
--        invoice_day_summary: already cut at Manila midnight, but their
--        "current_date" default was the UTC day
--    Each now runs with TimeZone = Asia/Manila (a per-function setting, so
--    nothing else in the database changes). Manila has no daylight saving.
--
-- 2. SCHEDULE WEEKS WERE SAVED AS SUNDAYS. The portal turned "Monday 00:00
--    local" into a date with toISOString(), which in UTC+8 is the Sunday
--    before. The portal now sends the Manila date (TPN.today), so rows
--    saved before have to move forward one day, or every saved schedule
--    would vanish from the screen. Production had 1 such row on Oct 25.
--    A Sunday row whose Monday already exists for that person is left.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

alter function public.finance_summary(date, date, text, uuid)        set timezone to 'Asia/Manila';
alter function public.inventory_variance(date, date, uuid)            set timezone to 'Asia/Manila';
alter function public.build_payroll_run(date, date, uuid)             set timezone to 'Asia/Manila';
alter function public.set_order_number()                              set timezone to 'Asia/Manila';
alter function public.daily_close_report(date, uuid)                  set timezone to 'Asia/Manila';
alter function public.record_daily_close(date, numeric, text, uuid)   set timezone to 'Asia/Manila';
alter function public.record_day_invoice(date, text, numeric, uuid)  set timezone to 'Asia/Manila';
alter function public.invoice_day_summary(date, uuid)                 set timezone to 'Asia/Manila';

update public.schedules s
   set week_start = s.week_start + 1
 where extract(dow from s.week_start) = 0
   and not exists (select 1 from public.schedules m
                    where m.staff_id = s.staff_id and m.week_start = s.week_start + 1);
````

<a id="59"></a>

## 59. 52-close-the-day.sql

`sql/52-close-the-day.sql` · 140 lines · md5 `b5fd9362a69bfc3f843856043d3b0289`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 52 — close the day: one checklist of what is still open (Oct 25, 2026)
--
-- Ending the day was four separate jobs in three places: close every
-- table (floor), each cashier's Z read (counter), the manager's count
-- (Daily close) and the end-of-day booklet invoice (Daily close). Nothing
-- said what was still missing, and the Rev 36 live test left a drawer open.
--
-- close_day_checklist(date, branch) answers it in one call, for the
-- Daily close screen:
--   open_orders   every order not completed or cancelled, from that day
--                 or any day before it (a table left open yesterday counts)
--   unpaid        orders of that day that are finished but not fully paid
--   open_drawers  cash shifts still open, with what the drawer should hold
--   z_reads       shifts closed that day, with their variance
--   pending_bank  bank transfers waiting for a second person
--   clocked_in    people still clocked in (closing the app without
--                 Switch user leaves them on the clock)
--   invoices      invoice_day_summary for that day (missing booklet
--                 invoices, small sales for the end-of-day invoice)
--   daily_close   the recorded count, if any
--   blocking      how many of the above still need someone
--
-- Read-only. finance.view (same as the Daily close itself). Day = Manila.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

create or replace function public.close_day_checklist(p_date date default null, p_branch uuid default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' set timezone to 'Asia/Manila'
as $$
declare
  v_branch uuid := coalesce(p_branch, private.my_branch());
  v_date   date := coalesce(p_date, current_date);
  v_lo     timestamptz := v_date::timestamptz;
  v_hi     timestamptz := (v_date + 1)::timestamptz;
  v_open   jsonb; v_unpaid jsonb; v_drawers jsonb; v_z jsonb; v_bank jsonb; v_clock jsonb;
  v_inv    jsonb; v_close jsonb; v_inv_left int;
begin
  if not private.can('finance.view') then
    raise exception 'permission_denied: finance.view' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_branch is distinct from private.my_branch() and not private.has_role('admin') then
    raise exception 'wrong_branch' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'id', o.id, 'order_number', o.order_number, 'order_type', o.order_type,
           'status', o.status, 'table_number', t.table_number, 'customer_name', o.customer_name,
           'total', o.total, 'payment_status', o.payment_status,
           'amount_due', private.amount_due(o.id), 'placed_at', o.placed_at)
           order by t.table_number nulls last, o.placed_at), '[]'::jsonb)
    into v_open
    from public.orders o left join public.restaurant_tables t on t.id = o.table_id
   where o.branch_id = v_branch and o.status not in ('completed', 'cancelled')
     and o.placed_at < v_hi;

  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'id', o.id, 'order_number', o.order_number, 'order_type', o.order_type,
           'customer_name', o.customer_name, 'total', o.total,
           'amount_due', private.amount_due(o.id),
           'bank_waiting', exists (select 1 from public.payments p where p.order_id = o.id
                                    and p.method = 'bank' and p.status = 'pending'))
           order by o.placed_at), '[]'::jsonb)
    into v_unpaid
    from public.orders o
   where o.branch_id = v_branch and o.status = 'completed'
     and o.payment_status <> 'paid' and o.total > 0
     and o.placed_at >= v_lo and o.placed_at < v_hi;

  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'id', s.id, 'register', s.register_label, 'opened_at', s.opened_at,
           'opened_by', st.full_name, 'opening_float', s.opening_float,
           'expected_cash', (public.cash_session_report(s.id) ->> 'expected_cash')::numeric)
           order by s.opened_at), '[]'::jsonb)
    into v_drawers
    from public.cash_sessions s left join public.staff st on st.id = s.opened_by
   where s.branch_id = v_branch and s.status = 'open';

  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'id', s.id, 'register', s.register_label, 'closed_at', s.closed_at,
           'closed_by', st.full_name, 'expected_cash', s.expected_cash,
           'counted_cash', s.counted_cash, 'variance', s.variance)
           order by s.closed_at), '[]'::jsonb)
    into v_z
    from public.cash_sessions s left join public.staff st on st.id = s.closed_by
   where s.branch_id = v_branch and s.status = 'closed'
     and s.closed_at >= v_lo and s.closed_at < v_hi;

  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'id', p.id, 'order_id', p.order_id, 'order_number', o.order_number,
           'amount', p.amount, 'reference', p.reference, 'created_at', p.created_at,
           'recorded_by', st.full_name, 'recorded_by_me', p.created_by = auth.uid())
           order by p.created_at), '[]'::jsonb)
    into v_bank
    from public.payments p
    join public.orders o on o.id = p.order_id
    left join public.staff st on st.id = p.created_by
   where p.branch_id = v_branch and p.method = 'bank' and p.status = 'pending';

  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'id', a.id, 'staff_id', a.staff_id, 'name', st.full_name, 'role', st.role,
           'clock_in_at', a.clock_in_at)
           order by a.clock_in_at), '[]'::jsonb)
    into v_clock
    from public.attendance a join public.staff st on st.id = a.staff_id
   where st.branch_id = v_branch and a.clock_out_at is null
     and a.clock_in_at < v_hi
     and st.role not in ('kitchen_display', 'dine_in_display');

  v_inv := public.invoice_day_summary(v_date, v_branch);
  v_inv_left := jsonb_array_length(coalesce(v_inv -> 'missing', '[]'::jsonb))
              + case when coalesce((v_inv ->> 'small_count')::int, 0) > 0
                      and jsonb_array_length(coalesce(v_inv -> 'day_invoices', '[]'::jsonb)) = 0
                     then 1 else 0 end;

  select jsonb_build_object('expected_cash', d.expected_cash, 'counted_cash', d.counted_cash,
                            'variance', d.variance, 'closed_at', d.closed_at, 'closed_by', st.full_name)
    into v_close
    from public.daily_closes d left join public.staff st on st.id = d.closed_by
   where d.branch_id = v_branch and d.close_date = v_date;

  return jsonb_build_object(
    'date', v_date,
    'is_today', v_date = current_date,
    'open_orders', v_open,
    'unpaid', v_unpaid,
    'open_drawers', v_drawers,
    'z_reads', v_z,
    'pending_bank', v_bank,
    'clocked_in', v_clock,
    'invoices', v_inv,
    'invoices_left', v_inv_left,
    'daily_close', v_close,
    'blocking', jsonb_array_length(v_open) + jsonb_array_length(v_unpaid)
              + jsonb_array_length(v_drawers) + jsonb_array_length(v_bank)
              + jsonb_array_length(v_clock) + v_inv_left
              + case when v_close is null then 1 else 0 end);
end $$;
revoke all on function public.close_day_checklist(date, uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.close_day_checklist(date, uuid) to authenticated;
````

<a id="60"></a>

## 60. 53-sold-out-stays-listed.sql

`sql/53-sold-out-stays-listed.sql` · 44 lines · md5 `3ae3ab80b344737ffb47dc39c1090015`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 53 — a sold-out dish stays on the menu, marked sold out (Oct 25, 2026)
--
-- menu_items.is_available meant two things: "sold out today" (the 86
-- button) and "not on the menu". The read policy (sql/01) returned only
-- available dishes, so:
--   • guests: an 86'd dish vanished from the website and the table QR menu
--     instead of showing "Sold out" (Rev 36 finding). Both pages already
--     draw sold-out cards; they never received any.
--   • kitchen and dining staff: the 86 list (Kitchen queue → Sold out, and
--     the floor's Menu tab) read the same table, so a dish a cook marked
--     sold out disappeared from that cook's own list and only a manager
--     could switch it back on.
--
-- Now two columns, two meanings:
--   is_listed     on the menu at all (Menu → edit dish → "On the menu").
--                 Off = gone from every menu. Managers only (menu_items_write).
--   is_available  available today. Off = sold out: still listed, greyed
--                 out, no add button. set_item_available (menu.86) as before.
-- Everyone reads listed dishes. submit_order still refuses a sold-out one.
-- On Oct 25 production had no unavailable dishes, so nothing moves.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

alter table public.menu_items add column if not exists is_listed boolean not null default true;
comment on column public.menu_items.is_listed is
  'On the menu at all. False hides the dish everywhere. is_available = false only marks it sold out (sql/53).';

drop policy if exists menu_items_read on public.menu_items;
create policy menu_items_read on public.menu_items
  for select to anon, authenticated using (is_listed);

-- submit_order checks is_available only. A dish taken off the menu is
-- never orderable (an old cart or a direct call could still name it), so
-- unlisting also switches it off; relisting leaves that to the manager.
create or replace function private.trg_menu_unlisted_unavailable()
returns trigger language plpgsql set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if not new.is_listed then new.is_available := false; end if;
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.trg_menu_unlisted_unavailable() from public, anon, authenticated;
drop trigger if exists trg_menu_unlisted_unavailable on public.menu_items;
create trigger trg_menu_unlisted_unavailable before insert or update of is_listed, is_available on public.menu_items
  for each row execute function private.trg_menu_unlisted_unavailable();
````

<a id="after-restore"></a>

## after-restore.sql

`ops/migration/after-restore.sql` · 85 lines · md5 `741c07ddea3bde5ae5791589a88c3d8a`

````sql
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- after-restore — run ONCE on the NEW Supabase project, after the
-- schema and data are in (MIGRATION.md, step B5). Safe to re-run.
--
-- A Supabase dump only carries the app's own schemas (public, private).
-- The pieces below live in Supabase-managed places (auth, storage,
-- the realtime publication) and are not copied by the dump, so they are
-- put back here exactly as the migrations made them:
--   1. extensions      pgcrypto + uuid-ossp in schema extensions
--   2. auth trigger    on_auth_user_created → public.handle_new_user (sql/01)
--   3. storage         private bucket attendance-photos + 3 policies (sql/33)
--   4. realtime        the 10 tables production broadcasts (Nov 7, 2026)
--   5. checks          one row per item, all should say ok = true
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- ── 1. Extensions ────────────────────────────────────────────────
create extension if not exists pgcrypto with schema extensions;
create extension if not exists "uuid-ossp" with schema extensions;

-- ── 2. New login → staff row (sql/01) ───────────────────────────
drop trigger if exists on_auth_user_created on auth.users;
create trigger on_auth_user_created
  after insert on auth.users
  for each row execute function public.handle_new_user();

-- ── 3. Clock-in photo bucket + policies (sql/33) ────────────────
insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
values ('attendance-photos', 'attendance-photos', false, 1048576, array['image/jpeg'])
on conflict (id) do update set public = false, file_size_limit = 1048576, allowed_mime_types = array['image/jpeg'];

drop policy if exists clockin_photo_upload on storage.objects;
create policy clockin_photo_upload on storage.objects for insert to authenticated
  with check (
    bucket_id = 'attendance-photos'
    and split_part(name, '/', 3) = auth.uid()::text
    and exists (select 1 from public.attendance a
                 where a.staff_id = auth.uid()
                   and name like '%/' || a.id::text || '.jpg'
                   and a.photo_path is null
                   and a.clock_in_at > now() - interval '10 minutes')
  );
drop policy if exists clockin_photo_read on storage.objects;
create policy clockin_photo_read on storage.objects for select to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'attendance-photos' and private.can('attendance.verify'));
drop policy if exists clockin_photo_delete on storage.objects;
create policy clockin_photo_delete on storage.objects for delete to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'attendance-photos' and private.can('attendance.verify'));

-- ── 4. Realtime: the tables the screens listen to ───────────────
do $$
declare t text;
begin
  foreach t in array array['attendance', 'inquiries', 'menu_items', 'messages', 'notifications',
                           'order_items', 'orders', 'payments', 'restaurant_tables', 'void_requests']
  loop
    if not exists (select 1 from pg_publication_tables
                    where pubname = 'supabase_realtime' and schemaname = 'public' and tablename = t) then
      execute format('alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.%I', t);
    end if;
  end loop;
end $$;

-- ── 5. Checks (every row should say ok = true) ──────────────────
select 'pgcrypto installed' as name, exists (select 1 from pg_extension where extname = 'pgcrypto') as ok
union all
select 'auth trigger', exists (select 1 from pg_trigger where tgname = 'on_auth_user_created')
union all
select 'photo bucket private', exists (select 1 from storage.buckets where id = 'attendance-photos' and not public)
union all
select 'photo policies = 3', (select count(*) from pg_policies where schemaname = 'storage' and policyname like 'clockin_photo_%') = 3
union all
select 'realtime tables = 10', (select count(*) from pg_publication_tables where pubname = 'supabase_realtime' and schemaname = 'public') >= 10
union all
select 'staff_role enum order', (select string_agg(e.enumlabel, ',' order by e.enumsortorder) from pg_enum e join pg_type t on t.oid = e.enumtypid where t.typname = 'staff_role')
  = 'kitchen_display,dine_in_display,dining,kitchen,supervisor,manager,admin,director,ceo'
union all
select 'manager PIN (sql/49)', to_regprocedure('public.pin_check(text, text)') is not null
union all
select 'menu engineering (sql/50)', to_regclass('public.menu_item_costs') is not null
union all
select 'close the day (sql/52)', exists (select 1 from pg_proc where proname = 'close_day_checklist')
union all
select 'sold out stays listed (sql/53)', exists (select 1 from information_schema.columns where table_schema = 'public' and table_name = 'menu_items' and column_name = 'is_listed')
union all
select 'stamp card secret kept', (select count(*) from private.loyalty_secret) > 0;
````

<a id="count-rows"></a>

## count-rows.sql

`ops/migration/count-rows.sql` · 12 lines · md5 `cfb3bf037cff3cdf307a24367dd39f09`

````sql
-- Exact row count of every table that moves (MIGRATION.md, steps B1 and B6).
-- Run it on the OLD project before the move and on the NEW one after it.
-- Save both results; every line must match. Read-only.
select table_schema || '.' || table_name as "table",
       (xpath('/row/c/text()',
              query_to_xml(format('select count(*) as c from %I.%I', table_schema, table_name), false, true, '')))[1]::text::bigint as "rows"
  from information_schema.tables
 where table_type = 'BASE TABLE'
   and (table_schema in ('public', 'private')
        or (table_schema, table_name) in (('auth', 'users'), ('auth', 'identities'),
                                          ('storage', 'buckets'), ('storage', 'objects')))
 order by 1;
````
